## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Dante - A

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Dante - B

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Dante - C

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Dante - A

Order Number: 1

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Dante - B

Order Number: 1

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Dante - C

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Dante - A

Dialogue: "What captivates me about the Showcase is not merely the inventions themselves, but the possibility that we are witnessing objects before history has decided they are important. Every celebrated artifact was contemporary once."

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Dante - B

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Dante - C

Dialogue: "I caught myself wondering which invention deserves a place in the Hall of Wonder someday. Then it occurred to me that perhaps I'm thinking too far ahead. They deserve an opportunity to change the present before I begin consigning them to history."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Dante - A

Dialogue: "Harvest celebrations predate nearly every institution in Blackmere. Buildings change, customs evolve, families come and go, yet people continue gathering around a table when the work is finished. There is something profoundly human about that continuity."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Dante - B

Dialogue: "Future historians could learn quite a bit from this table: what Blackmere cultivated, how dishes were prepared, which ingredients were abundant. Of course, analyzing supper while everyone else is eating it may be taking professional curiosity somewhat too far."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Dante - C

Dialogue: "We often preserve extraordinary moments and neglect ordinary ones. Yet I suspect evenings like this...a crowded table, familiar voices, everyone exhausted and content...are precisely the moments people eventually wish they could revisit."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Dante - A

Dialogue: "Graythorne Lake possesses an astonishing collection of folklore. Apparitions beneath the surface, inexplicable lights, vanished travelers, impossible creatures...most emerged during entirely different periods, yet people have woven them together into one wonderfully inconsistent mythology."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Dante - B

Dialogue: "I could explain why moonlight distorts one's perception of distance across dark water. I could also explain why sounds become difficult to place along the shoreline. Curiously, neither explanation has made Graythorne Lake feel any less ominous tonight."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Dante - C

Dialogue: "There is a temptation, particularly among historians, to separate fact from folklore and discard whatever cannot be substantiated. I think that misses the point. A story needn't be true to tell us something truthful about the people who kept telling it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Dante - A

Dialogue: "Museums preserve objects because objects survive when people cannot. A photograph, a letter, an ordinary possession...suddenly they carry an importance their owner never anticipated. Today makes the responsibility of preserving them feel considerably heavier."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Dante - B

Dialogue: "The flood will inevitably become history to generations who never experienced it. I hope when that happens, we preserve more than dates and statistics. Those tell us what occurred. They cannot, by themselves, tell us whom Blackmere lost."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Dante - C

Dialogue: "There are moments when silence preserves something better than explanation ever could. For someone in my profession, admitting that is...uncharacteristically difficult. But today, I believe it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Dante - A

Dialogue: "Agricultural societies have celebrated planting seasons for thousands of years. The particulars differ enormously, naturally, but the sentiment remains recognizable: hope expressed through the rather audacious act of burying something valuable and trusting it to return."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Dante - B

Dialogue: "Seedwake celebrates something that has not happened yet. No harvest exists, no success is guaranteed, and nevertheless everyone gathers in anticipation. Perhaps optimism has always required a certain willingness to celebrate uncertainty."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Dante - C

Dialogue: "I asked Jonas whether the meal traditionally occurred before or after the first planting. His answer was considerably simpler than my research: 'When everyone's hungry.' There are occasions when oral history lacks the precision one hopes for."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Dante - A

Dialogue: "There are surviving descriptions of Snow Bell's Eve from well over a century ago. The music has changed, the Square has changed, even the dances have changed. Yet every account describes people lingering because nobody wishes to be the first to admit the year is truly over."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Dante - B

Dialogue: "People speak of the new year as though midnight creates a clean division between past and future. Historically speaking, that's nonsense. Emotionally speaking...I concede there is something rather compelling about it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Dante - C

Dialogue: "I'm a competent dancer in theory. I understand rhythm, sequence, timing, and the historical development of several forms. Unfortunately, none of that appears to have informed my feet."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Dante - A

Dialogue: "Pendrelle Manor is usually discussed in terms of architecture, lineage, and historical significance. Tonight offers a useful reminder that grand houses were never merely structures. They were places where people ate too much, interrupted one another, laughed loudly, and lived."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Dante - B

Dialogue: "One could document every tradition observed tonight and still fail to explain why the evening matters. Perhaps that's because traditions are merely the framework. The people gathered within them provide the meaning."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Dante - C

Dialogue: "I confess I've spent an embarrassing portion of the evening examining the manor's historical details. Then someone handed me a plate and insisted I stop curating the holiday. It was sound advice."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Dante - A

Dialogue: "Human beings have spent centuries attaching romantic significance to celestial phenomena. Ordinarily, I would caution against allowing poetry to obscure scientific understanding. Tonight...I find myself considerably less interested in making that distinction."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Dante - B

Dialogue: "The aurora is magnificent on its own, certainly. Yet I suspect what people remember years afterward isn't merely the color of the sky. They remember whose voice they heard beside them when they looked up."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Dante - C

Dialogue: "Museums are filled with attempts to preserve beautiful moments: paintings, poems, photographs, keepsakes. Standing here, I understand the impulse rather intimately. Some evenings make you wish preservation were as simple as deciding not to let them end."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Dante - A

Dialogue: "Recipes are fascinating historical documents. Ingredients reveal trade, preparation reveals technology, and alterations reveal cultural exchange. This particular recipe also reveals that I apparently possess no intuitive understanding whatsoever of when dough has been kneaded sufficiently."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Dante - B

Dialogue: "Helen said the recipe has been passed down for years, so naturally I asked how many years, whether the ingredients had changed, and who first recorded it. She handed me a spoon and told me to stir."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Dante - C

Dialogue: "There is something wonderfully democratic about learning a recipe this way. Knowledge isn't placed behind glass or restricted to a written archive. Someone simply stands beside you and says, 'Here. This is how we make it.'"

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Dante - A

Dialogue: "Inns have historically functioned as extraordinary centers of information. Travelers exchange news, rumors, customs, and stories over meals much like this one. Admittedly, most people here seem more interested in breakfast than the anthropological significance of breakfast."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Dante - B

Dialogue: "Frederick has created something institutions frequently struggle to achieve: a place where people feel comfortable remaining after they have technically finished what they came to do. I suspect that's why nobody seems eager to leave."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Dante - C

Dialogue: "I intended to have breakfast and return promptly to the Hall of Wonder. That was quite some time ago. I suppose there are worse reasons for a curator to neglect his exhibits."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Dante - A

Dialogue: "Family homes preserve history differently from museums. There are no plaques explaining the scratches on a table or photographs documenting every repaired chair. Everyone simply knows the stories. I find that form of preservation rather beautiful."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-dante-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Dante - B

Dialogue: "I asked about one of the older pieces in the house and received four different explanations from three Mercers. None agreed. This is precisely why oral histories are simultaneously invaluable and infuriating."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-dante-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Dante - C

Dialogue: "The Hall of Wonder preserves Blackmere's remarkable things. Evenings like this remind me that perhaps I've defined 'remarkable' too narrowly. A meal doesn't need historical significance to become something worth remembering."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-dante-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Dante Menici

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Leo - A

Dialogue: "Look around! Fabrics I've never worked with, dyes I've never seen, patterns I couldn't have dreamed up if you gave me a month. I may need someone to physically drag me home when this fair ends."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Leo - B

Dialogue: "That merchant over there is wearing the most extraordinary coat. The cut, the embroidery, those buttons! I promised myself I wouldn't interrogate strangers about their clothes today, but that promise is weakening."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Leo - C

Dialogue: "This is why I adore the Trade Fair. You arrive thinking you know what you like, then somebody from halfway across the world shows you something completely different and suddenly your imagination has somewhere new to go."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Leo - A

Dialogue: "Before you say anything, yes, I chose this outfit knowing there would be water involved. Looking fabulous and making sensible decisions are occasionally incompatible."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Leo - B

Dialogue: "Oh, don't give me that innocent look. You've got a bucket, I've got a bucket, and we're standing within splashing distance. We both know how this ends."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Leo - C

Dialogue: "My hair is ruined, my shoes are soaked, and somebody dumped an entire bucket down my back. I'm having a wonderful time."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Leo - A

Dialogue: "You can tell so much about an inventor from what they make. Some want to solve problems, some want to impress people, and some clearly woke up one morning and thought, 'Wouldn't this be fun?' I appreciate that last group."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Leo - B

Dialogue: "Function matters, obviously, but presentation matters too! If you've spent months creating something extraordinary, give it a little drama when you unveil it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Leo - C

Dialogue: "I love watching people explain something they've created. There's always that moment when they forget everyone's watching and you can see exactly how much the idea means to them."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Leo - A

Dialogue: "Autumn colors, lantern light, a table overflowing with food...the Mercers could put absolutely no effort into decorating tonight and somehow everything would still look perfect."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Leo - B

Dialogue: "Everyone looks exhausted and ridiculously pleased with themselves. As they should! You don't finish an entire harvest and pretend it's just another day."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Leo - C

Dialogue: "I've already eaten too much, and I haven't tried half the table. This is becoming a logistical problem."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Leo - A

Dialogue: "Graythorne Lake is gorgeous tonight in a deeply unsettling sort of way. The moonlight, the mist, the black water...I wouldn't want to be here alone, but I can't stop looking."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Leo - B

Dialogue: "Someone told me not to wear anything that could snag if I had to run from something. Excuse me? That warning raised several questions I would have preferred not to have."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Leo - C

Dialogue: "I'm perfectly happy listening to spooky stories beside the lake. I simply request that whatever we're telling stories about remains politely inside the stories."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Leo - A

Dialogue: "I almost wore something brighter today, then wondered if that would be inappropriate. But remembrance doesn't have to be colorless, does it? People were more than the way we lost them."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Leo - B

Dialogue: "There are people here comforting each other without saying a word. A hand on someone's shoulder, standing a little closer...sometimes that's enough."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Leo - C

Dialogue: "Blackmere lost so much in the flood. I think continuing to make beautiful things here is one way of saying that loss doesn't get to be the end of our story."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Leo - A

Dialogue: "Everything feels so hopeful today! Fresh fields, spring clothes, flowers coming back...honestly, the entire season has excellent taste."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Leo - B

Dialogue: "I asked what everyone plans to grow this year, and somehow I've ended up planning an entire collection around it. Apparently we're doing agricultural fashion now."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Leo - C

Dialogue: "Beginnings are wonderful because nobody expects them to look finished yet. Everything gets a little room to become whatever it's going to be."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Leo - A

Dialogue: "Now THIS is an occasion to dress for! Dancing, lights, snow, an entire town pretending they're not checking what everyone else decided to wear. Delightful."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Leo - B

Dialogue: "You're dancing tonight. That's not a question. Nobody remembers whether you knew the steps; they remember whether you looked like you were enjoying yourself."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Leo - C

Dialogue: "I always get a little sentimental around midnight. One minute everyone's laughing about the year we had, and the next we're standing together wondering who we'll be by this time next year."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Leo - A

Dialogue: "Oh, Pendrelle Manor was MADE for this! Warm lights, crowded rooms, everyone dressed for the holidays...you could put this evening on a card."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Leo - B

Dialogue: "Half the fun of a holiday gathering is seeing what everyone wears when they know the entire town will be together. Don't judge me. People-watching is practically professional development."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Leo - C

Dialogue: "A house this grand could feel terribly formal with the wrong crowd. Tonight it just feels warm. I think that's quite an achievement."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Leo - A

Dialogue: "All right, this is unfairly romantic. The stars, the aurora, everyone huddled together against the cold...someone clearly designed this evening to make people fall for each other."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Leo - B

Dialogue: "Look at everyone pretending they're here exclusively for the aurora. Please. Half of them have barely looked at the sky."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Leo - C

Dialogue: "Beautiful things feel different when you share them with someone, don't they? You remember the view, of course...but you remember the company too."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Leo - A

Dialogue: "Helen told me presentation comes after flavor. She's correct, obviously. That doesn't mean I'm serving something ugly."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Leo - B

Dialogue: "Cooking and tailoring have more in common than you'd think. Good materials, careful measurements, knowing when to follow the pattern...and knowing when the pattern desperately needs improving."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Leo - C

Dialogue: "Theo tasted mine, went quiet for a moment, and then said, 'Interesting.' Interesting! Do you know how terrifying that word is without further explanation?"

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Leo - A

Dialogue: "Who decided we should all meet while half the town is still waking up? Actually, never mind. There's coffee. I withdraw the complaint."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Leo - B

Dialogue: "Morning light is terribly revealing. Somehow everyone still looks charming. Maybe Blackmere is just unfairly photogenic."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Leo - C

Dialogue: "This is my favorite kind of gathering. Nobody's trying too hard yet. Messy hair, second cups of coffee, conversations wandering wherever they please...everyone feels wonderfully unpolished."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-leo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Leo - A

Dialogue: "The Mercers have that wonderful kind of table where nobody stays in their original seat. Give it another half hour and we'll all have moved twice."

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-leo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Leo - B

Dialogue: "I complimented Claudia's shirt and somehow that turned into a discussion about whether farm clothes need to look good. For the record: yes. Obviously."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-leo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Leo - C

Dialogue: "Listen to this room! Someone's laughing, someone else is telling a story, Milo is talking far too loudly...it's chaotic, affectionate, and completely impossible to manufacture."

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Leo Finch

Variant: C

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Madison - A

Dialogue: "Crowds are steady, the main paths are clear, and the traders seem settled. Good. Perhaps I can actually see what they're selling now."

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Madison - B

Dialogue: "Three days of unfamiliar merchants and heavier foot traffic keeps me busy. Still, seeing the Square this full of life is worth the extra patrols."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-madison-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Madison - C

Dialogue: "I've met travelers from four different regions today. Most came here expecting to sell something. Several already seem more interested in Blackmere itself."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-madison-c

Order Number: 1

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Madison - A

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Madison - B

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Madison - C

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Madison - A

Dialogue: "A clever invention is impressive. A clever invention that's dependable when someone actually needs it? That's the one that gets my attention."

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Madison - B

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Madison - C

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Madison - A

Dialogue: "Harvest is finished. Equipment is stored, fields are settling, and nobody needs anything from me at the moment. I'm trying not to be suspicious of that last part."

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Madison - B

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-madison-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Madison - C

Dialogue: "Beatrice told me I'm forbidden from turning supper into an informal town meeting. She's right. Don't tell her I admitted that."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-madison-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Madison - A

Dialogue: "Graythorne Lake is safe tonight. I've checked the shoreline myself. That statement does not apply to whatever creatures people insist are lurking beneath it."

Event: Festivals

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Madison - B

Dialogue: "Stay where the shore is visible and mind the rocks after dark. Enjoy the stories, certainly. Just don't let one convince you to wander into the woods alone."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-madison-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Madison - C

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-madison-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Madison - A

Dialogue: "Ian protected this town for years. I won't pretend I knew him the way many people here did. I know what he gave Blackmere, though."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-madison-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Madison - B

Dialogue: "During the flood, there was always another person to find, another road to secure, another decision to make. We didn't have time to understand everything we'd lost. Today gives us that time."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-madison-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Madison - C

Dialogue: "Protecting Blackmere means remembering the people who protected it before me. I carry that responsibility every day. Today, I simply carry it more openly."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-madison-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Madison - A

Dialogue: "A new growing season means longer workdays and more activity along the outer paths. For now, though, everyone is here. That's worth enjoying."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-madison-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Madison - B

Dialogue: "There's confidence in planting a field before you know what the season will bring. You prepare properly, account for what you can, then move forward."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-madison-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Madison - C

Dialogue: "Jonas has everything under control. Claudia has everything under control. The rest of the Mercers have everything under control. I'm repeating that until I believe I don't need to help."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-madison-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Madison - A

Dialogue: "Town Square is secure, the weather is holding, and nobody has required my attention for nearly an hour. I'd call that an excellent start to the evening."

Event: Festivals

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Madison - B

Dialogue: "I don't put much faith in resolutions. Decide what matters, then do the work. Still...a new year is as good a reason as any to begin."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-madison-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Madison - C

Event: Festivals

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Madison - A

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Madison - B

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Madison - C

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: C

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Madison - A

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Madison - B

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Madison - C

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Madison Remington

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Mara - C

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Mara - A

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Mara - B

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Mara - C

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Mara - A

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Mara - B

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Mara - C

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Mara - A

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Mara - B

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Mara - C

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Mara - A

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Mara - B

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Mara - C

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: C

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Mara - A

Dialogue: "After the flood, everybody wanted things rebuilt immediately. Couldn't blame them. But some things need time before you can even figure out where to start."

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Mara - B

Dialogue: "I've replaced walls, floors, roofs...plenty of what the flood took. There are losses you can't rebuild. Best you can do is make sure they aren't forgotten."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-mara-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Mara - C

Dialogue: "Blackmere's still standing because people kept showing up for each other. Worth remembering that along with everything else today."

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Mara - A

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Mara - B

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Mara - C

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Mara - A

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Mara - C

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Mara - A

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Mara - B

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Mara - C

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: C

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Mara - A

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Mara - B

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Mara - C

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Mara Klein

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Nora - A

Speaker: Nora Reed

Speaker: Nora Reed

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "Some contestants keep glancing at the judges instead of their inventions. I wish they could see what I see: they imagined something that didn't exist, then found a way to make it real. A score can't take that away."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "I tell my students that mistakes aren't evidence that they can't do something. They're evidence that they're still learning how. Looking around today, I suspect inventors need that reminder too."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "There are so many different ideas here! Practical ones, ambitious ones, wonderfully strange ones...it's reassuring, somehow. There's never only one way to imagine a better future."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "I've heard people thanking the Mercers all evening, but I hope they're proud of themselves too. Sometimes the people who work hardest are the last ones to stop and acknowledge what they've accomplished."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "Milo has explained the entire harvest to me twice tonight. His version includes considerably more adventure than Jonas's, but I admire his enthusiasm."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "A harvest is made from hundreds of small things done properly over months. Watering, tending, waiting, trying again. It's nice that we celebrate the result without forgetting all the little efforts that created it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "Felix asked whether any of the stories about Graythorne Lake are true. I told him folklore doesn't always need to be factual to matter. He informed me that wasn't remotely what he was asking."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "The lake really does look different tonight, doesn't it? Familiar places can become wonderfully strange with nothing more than darkness, moonlight, and a few good stories."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "I've spent half the evening reassuring children that there are no monsters in Graythorne Lake. Nora has spent the other half convincing them there are. We may need to discuss professional boundaries."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "After the flood, I was so focused on making everything feel normal for the children that I rarely stopped to ask whether normal was what they needed. Sometimes people need permission to be sad before they're ready to feel hopeful again."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "Some of my students remember the flood clearly. Others were too young, and some only know it through the people around them. I want all of them to understand why today matters without teaching them that Blackmere is defined by what happened."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "We can't make grief disappear for someone. I used to think finding the right words might do that. Now I think sitting beside them and letting them know they aren't alone can be enough."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "The children planted seeds at school recently. Every morning, at least one of them checks whether anything has sprouted yet. Patience is apparently much easier to teach than to practice."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "There's something lovely about celebrating before anyone knows how the season will turn out. It says we're allowed to be hopeful without requiring a guarantee first."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "Kira has already made predictions about which crops will grow best this year. Milo's prediction was 'the biggest ones.' I suppose we'll know eventually which method was more accurate."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "The children keep talking about everything they'll accomplish next year. I hope they never lose that certainty that another year means another chance to become something new."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "I used to make very ambitious resolutions. Read more, work harder, become a better teacher...this year, I think I'd like to become better at recognizing when I've already done enough."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "There's always a moment before midnight when everyone seems to look around at the people beside them. I think that's my favorite part. Before we wonder what the next year brings, we remember who made this one matter."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "I brought a few books in case the children needed something quiet to do. So far they've been far too busy enjoying themselves to notice. I'm very pleased to have been unnecessary."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "Pendrelle Manor feels different tonight. Not because of the decorations, though they're beautiful. I think it's hearing so many people feel at home in the same place."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "Holidays can be difficult for people who are missing someone. I'm glad tonight leaves room for both things...to feel the absence and still enjoy the people who are here."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "It's beautiful, isn't it? Though I think I'd remember tonight even without the aurora. Sometimes the person sharing a moment with you becomes the part that makes it special."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "Everyone seems a little quieter up here. Perhaps it's the view...or perhaps being beside someone you care about makes silence feel less like something that needs filling."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "I spend so much time hoping other people recognize how extraordinary they are. Nights like this remind me how lovely it feels when someone looks at you as though they already know."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "One of the nicest things about teaching is watching the moment something finally makes sense to someone. I hadn't realized how satisfying it would be to experience that from the other side of the lesson."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "I apologized to Helen because mine didn't look quite right. She asked whether it tasted good. When I said yes, she told me to stop inventing problems. I may borrow that lesson."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "Theo is a very patient teacher. He lets you make the mistake, waits until you notice it yourself, then helps you understand why it happened. I should remember that."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "I thought breakfast would be quieter than the other gatherings. Then the children arrived. I don't know why I expected otherwise."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "There's something comforting about seeing everyone before the day has properly begun. Nobody has accomplished anything yet. Nobody needs to. We're simply here together."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "Frederick asked whether I was enjoying myself, and I immediately started telling him how much the children were enjoying themselves. He asked again. Apparently I have a habit."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Sabrina - A

Dialogue: "The Mercer children have spent most of supper interrupting one another because they're all desperate to tell the best story first. I probably shouldn't encourage it, but they're making me laugh too much."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-sabrina-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Sabrina - B

Dialogue: "I like seeing my students at home. In the classroom, I'm responsible for helping them grow. Here, I get to see all the people who've been helping them grow long before they ever reach me."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-sabrina-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Sabrina - C

Dialogue: "Jonas keeps making sure everyone has enough, Claudia keeps helping without being asked, and the children keep making the room louder. It's easy to understand why this house feels so warm."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-sabrina-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Sabrina Fairchild

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Victor - A

Dialogue: "Now this feels familiar. Different accents, unfamiliar goods, merchants talking with their hands because neither side knows the other's language...give me a crowded market and five minutes, and I feel right at home."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Victor - B

Dialogue: "I've crossed paths with two of these caravans before. One remembers me fondly. The other remembers that I won six rounds of cards and left with considerably more coin than I arrived with. Best not ask which is which."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Victor - C

Dialogue: "Interesting, isn't it? Traders arrive thinking they're bringing the world to Blackmere. Then they start asking about the town, buying local goods, swapping stories...by the time they leave, Blackmere goes back out into the world with them."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Victor - A

Dialogue: "I once attended a summer festival where everyone threw colored powder at one another. Beautiful tradition. Took three days to get it out of my hair. Water is an improvement."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Victor - B

Dialogue: "Here's the mistake everyone makes: they watch the person carrying the bucket. Watch the person standing near the water instead. That's the one planning their next move."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Victor - C

Dialogue: "You're looking remarkably dry. Either you're very good at this or you've been hiding. Come on, which is it?"

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Victor - A

Dialogue: "I've seen clever inventions disappear because nobody outside one little village ever knew they existed. That's what I like about this. Build something worthwhile here, and eventually someone like me might carry the idea somewhere it can help somebody else."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Victor - B

Dialogue: "Never underestimate an invention because it solves a small problem. Travelers pay very good money for anything that makes one annoying part of the day disappear."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Victor - C

Dialogue: "You know what I'd judge? Whether I could explain the invention to a stranger in another town and make them say, 'Where can I get one?' That's when you've made something interesting."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Victor - A

Dialogue: "Harvest suppers are everywhere if you travel long enough. Different dishes, different customs, different reasons why somebody's grandmother insists you're eating everything incorrectly. Somehow they all feel familiar."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Victor - B

Dialogue: "The Mercers put an empty chair at the table and someone immediately finds their way into it. That's a rare talent. Some places welcome visitors politely. This family makes you forget you're visiting."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Victor - C

Dialogue: "I've eaten meals in places I never saw again and with people whose names I've forgotten. Strange how certain tables stay with you anyway. I suspect this will be one of them."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Victor - A

Dialogue: "Every region has a lake people swear is haunted. Usually there's a drowned bell, a vanished traveler, or something enormous beneath the water. Graythorne has enough stories to cover all three, which feels a little greedy."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Victor - B

Dialogue: "See that light across Graythorne Lake? I've heard three explanations tonight. Lantern, ghost, and mysterious creature. I'm hoping for a fourth before we leave."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Victor - C

Dialogue: "A good traveler learns not to dismiss local warnings too quickly. Ninety-nine times out of a hundred, they're superstition. That remaining one is why you listen."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Victor - A

Dialogue: "I arrived in Blackmere after the flood, so there are names spoken today attached to people I never had the chance to meet. I've heard their stories often enough that some of them don't feel entirely like strangers anymore."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Victor - B

Dialogue: "Travel teaches you how quickly places change. You leave a town, return years later, and discover someone or something you remember is gone. Blackmere had that lesson forced upon it all at once."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Victor - C

Dialogue: "I used to think remembering a place meant remembering what it looked like. I've changed my mind. It's the people. Lose them, and even familiar roads lead somewhere different."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Victor - A

Dialogue: "I once visited a village where they buried a coin beneath the first planted row for good fortune. Jonas declined when I offered him one. Sensible farmer. Terrible customer."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Victor - B

Dialogue: "Beginning a growing season is a bit like setting out on a road you've never traveled. You prepare, make your best guess about what's ahead, and eventually discover how wrong the map was."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Victor - C

Dialogue: "There's something infectious about everyone talking about what might grow this year. Makes me want to head somewhere I've never been and see what turns up. Then again...I'm enjoying being here too."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Victor - A

Dialogue: "I've welcomed the new year in crowded cities, tiny villages, roadside camps, once aboard a caravan with a broken axle...never the same place twice for very long. Blackmere may be ruining that record."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Victor - B

Dialogue: "Midnight always makes people talk about where they're going next. Funny. This year I keep catching myself thinking about what I'd miss if I went."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Victor - C

Dialogue: "No resolutions for me. Too binding. I'll make a suggestion instead: another year of interesting roads, unexpected opportunities, and enough good company to make coming back worthwhile."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Victor - A

Dialogue: "I've spent winter holidays in inns, caravans, camps, and once beneath an overturned wagon during a snowstorm. A warm manor filled with friends is considerably better. Don't tell the road I said that."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Victor - B

Dialogue: "Most places have some version of this. Different food, different decorations, different traditions, but eventually someone pulls another chair closer because one more person showed up. I think that's the part that matters."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Victor - C

Dialogue: "Careful with evenings like this. Warm fire, good food, familiar faces...a traveler could get dangerously comfortable."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Victor - A

Dialogue: "I've watched the aurora from lonely roads where the nearest person was miles away. Beautiful, certainly. Turns out the view improves when there's someone beside you worth sharing it with."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Victor - B

Dialogue: "Dangerous sort of evening, this. Beautiful sky, quiet conversation, someone standing just a little closer because it's cold...next thing you know, a person starts reconsidering how appealing the open road really is."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Victor - C

Dialogue: "People assume travelers remember destinations. Sometimes. More often, I remember who was there. The right company has a way of becoming part of the scenery."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Victor - A

Dialogue: "I collect recipes when I travel. Not because I cook them particularly well, mind you. Food is simply one of the easiest ways to carry a place home with you."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Victor - B

Dialogue: "Helen asked me to follow the recipe exactly. I asked whether she'd consider ingredients from three other regions. She looked at me until I put them away."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Victor - C

Dialogue: "Theo showed me one little trick I've never seen anywhere else. That's the treasure people overlook. Gold travels easily. Knowledge like that takes generations to create."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Victor - A

Dialogue: "You learn a town quickly by eating breakfast at its inn. Who wakes early, who arrives late, who takes the same seat every morning...give me a week and I could probably draw you a social map."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Victor - B

Dialogue: "Traveler's rule: never refuse a good breakfast when somebody else is cooking. You don't know when the next one will be. Admittedly, that rule is less urgent when you live five minutes away."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Victor - C

Dialogue: "Strange thing about staying somewhere long enough: eventually you stop noticing which faces are familiar until one of them is missing. Looking around this room...I know nearly everyone now."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Victor - A

Dialogue: "I've learned an important rule about visiting the Mercers: arrive hungry and never claim you're full unless you're prepared to defend the statement."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-victor-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Victor - B

Dialogue: "I've eaten with families all over the map. You can always tell when you're still considered a guest. Somebody asks permission before handing you another plate. The Mercers stopped asking me quite some time ago."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-victor-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Victor - C

Dialogue: "This is how places get you, you know. First you're passing through. Then someone saves you a seat. Then one evening you realize you would've noticed if they hadn't."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-victor-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Victor Cross

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "The first traders who came through Blackmere carried everything in two wagons. Now they require half the Town Square. Progress does have a habit of taking up space."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "Do not buy something merely because it came from somewhere you've never heard of. Examine the workmanship first. A foreign piece of rubbish remains rubbish."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "I approve of the Fair. Blackmere should know something of the world beyond its borders, and the world could stand to know something of Blackmere."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "When I was young, we managed to enjoy summer without throwing buckets of water at one another. We were apparently deprived."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "I have informed the children that splashing me would be profoundly unwise. Whether they possess the judgment to heed that warning remains to be seen."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "Go on, enjoy yourself. There will be plenty of opportunities to behave respectably when you're older. I should know."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "I am judging what was built, not how charmingly someone explains it. If an invention requires a persuasive speech before it appears useful, we already have a problem."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "Originality is admirable. Reliability is better. Blackmere needs inventions that continue working after everyone has finished applauding them."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "Some people assume an old woman dislikes new ideas. Nonsense. I dislike bad ideas. Bring me a good one and I'll be the first to say so."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "Harvest suppers were smaller once. So were the harvests. I prefer having enough food to require another table."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "A successful harvest deserves celebration, but remember what you're celebrating. Months of discipline came before this meal. Good fortune only carries a farm so far."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "Jonas keeps insisting I take another helping. I have known that man long enough to understand resistance is a waste of everyone's time."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "Graythorne Lake had unsettling stories attached to it before most of you were born. Half are nonsense. The trouble is remembering which half."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "No, I will not tell you whether I believe the old stories about this lake. A little uncertainty improves the evening."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "People have always enjoyed frightening themselves in perfectly safe surroundings. It is considerably less amusing when you've lived long enough to know the genuinely frightening stories."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "Do not remember them only for how they died. Speak their names. Tell their stories. Remember what made them laugh. Otherwise you're preserving the tragedy and losing the person."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "I've watched Blackmere bury people before, and I expect I've attended more memorials than anyone should. Grief changes with age. It does not become easier. You simply learn how to carry it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "One day, nobody alive will remember the flood firsthand. That is precisely why today matters. Memory must eventually become history, and history must be entrusted to someone."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "People enjoy romanticizing the beginning of a growing season. Seeds, sunshine, possibilities. Ask them again when the weeds arrive."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "We celebrated the first planting even when Blackmere barely produced enough to feed itself. Perhaps especially then. Hope is most useful when circumstances haven't earned it yet."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "Another season begins. Plant carefully, work diligently, and don't complain about the weather as though it has any intention of listening."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "I've attended enough Snow Bell's Eves to know everyone becomes philosophical shortly before midnight. By morning, most of them return to their usual foolishness."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "Roland still asks me to dance every year. I still remind him my knees are older than his enthusiasm. Somehow, we manage."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "A new year does not make you a new person. If you want something to change, change it. Midnight is merely a convenient starting bell."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "Pendrelle Manor has seen more winters than most people at this table. Houses endure because each generation decides they're still worth caring for. Traditions are much the same."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "People fuss too much over making holidays perfect. Feed everyone properly, keep the fire warm, and make room for whoever arrives. The rest is decoration."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "Some traditions survive exactly as they began. Others change until you'd scarcely recognize them. I've spent years objecting to the latter. I'm beginning to suspect I may have wasted some excellent arguments."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "Roland and I watched the aurora together when we were young. We were considerably less sensible then. The view hasn't changed much. Fortunately, we have."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "Young people believe romance requires grand declarations. It doesn't. Find someone whose company remains welcome after fifty years of ordinary evenings. Then you'll have something worth boasting about."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "If there's someone you hoped would stand beside you tonight, stop staring at the sky long enough to appreciate that they're here. The aurora will return. People are not guaranteed the same courtesy."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "Helen asked whether I wanted the written recipe. I told her I've made variations of this dish since before she could reach a stove. She gave me the recipe anyway. Sensible woman."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "Write these recipes down. 'Everyone knows how to make it' is how perfectly good traditions disappear in a single generation."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "Theo changed one of the old recipes. I prepared myself to object. Then I tasted it. One must know when evidence has defeated principle."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Agatha - A

Dialogue: "A proper breakfast before a day's work used to be necessity, not an event. Apparently we've improved our circumstances enough to celebrate eating eggs."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "The Inn has always been one of the places where Blackmere becomes a community rather than a collection of houses. People talk differently when they're sharing a table."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "Frederick asked whether everything was satisfactory. I told him the tea was excellent and the eggs slightly overdone. He should know better than to ask me a question unless he wants the answer."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Agatha - A
