Dialogue: "The Mercer children interrupt too much, the adults encourage them, and nobody waits for the room to become quiet before speaking. It's a dreadful system. Rather pleasant, though."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-agatha-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Agatha - B

Dialogue: "I've watched generations sit around tables like this. Different faces, same arguments over portions, manners, and who ought to clear the dishes. Continuity is rarely as dignified as historians make it sound."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-agatha-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Agatha - C

Dialogue: "A town survives because people keep doing ordinary things together. Sharing meals. Raising children. Helping neighbors. Grand speeches are optional."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-agatha-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Agatha Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "Every occupied stall means another trader believed Blackmere was worth the journey. After everything this town has endured, I don't take that confidence lightly."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "The Trade Fair requires months of correspondence, permits, arrangements, and contingency planning. Then the gates open, the Square fills, and nobody sees any of it. That's precisely how it should be."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "I've already heard three merchants asking whether they should return next year. They're still unpacking. I'll consider that a promising sign."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "Apparently being mayor offers no immunity from a bucket of water. Several people have taken great satisfaction in demonstrating that."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "I considered remaining outside the competition. Then Kira informed me that would be 'extremely boring.' Her argument was unexpectedly persuasive."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "No speeches, no schedules, no decisions requiring my approval. Just half the town running through the forest with buckets. I admit there's something refreshing about it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "As a judge, I'm interested in more than ingenuity. Show me what your invention contributes to people's lives. Innovation should have a purpose beyond proving it can be done."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "Blackmere was rebuilt through people finding solutions to problems we never expected to face. I suppose that's why I take this competition rather seriously."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "Don't look at the judges while we're scoring. Look at what you built. Whatever number we give you, the work remains yours."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "A good harvest affects far more than Mercer Farms. Shops remain supplied, families remain secure, and another winter becomes easier to face. Tonight's celebration is well earned."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "Jonas asked me to stop thanking him on behalf of Blackmere and simply eat my supper. I believe that was his polite way of telling me I'm off duty."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "At the beginning of every growing season, there are uncertainties we can't control. Reaching this table together is never something I consider guaranteed."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "Graythorne Lake certainly knows how to look ominous when given the proper lighting. I can understand why so many stories have accumulated around it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "My mother knows several old stories about this lake that I've never found in the town archives. Whether that makes them more authentic or less reliable depends entirely on whom you ask."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "There is no official municipal position regarding monsters in Graythorne Lake. I cannot believe I have needed to clarify that twice tonight."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "Every name here belonged to someone Blackmere should still have. I can give speeches about courage and remembrance, but no words have ever made that easier to accept."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "I used to believe honoring the people we lost meant rebuilding everything they left behind. I'm beginning to understand that we honor them by building something worthy of those who remain."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "There are decisions I made during the flood that I still revisit. Roads closed. Resources moved. People sent one direction instead of another. Today makes it difficult not to wonder what one different decision might have changed."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "Seedwake always feels like Blackmere making a promise to itself. We may not know what the season will bring, but we're willing to plant something anyway."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "There are forecasts, planting schedules, supply estimates, and contingency plans waiting on my desk. None of them can guarantee a successful season. Some things still require faith in the work ahead."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "Everyone seems particularly hopeful this morning. I won't interrupt that with administrative concerns. They can wait until tomorrow."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "At midnight, everyone will cheer for the year ahead. For once, I intend to join them before thinking about everything that needs doing in it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "Town Hall closes for one evening and suddenly people are surprised to discover I know how to dance. I did have a life before public office."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "Every year I write down what Blackmere accomplished and what remains unfinished. Tonight, I'm trying to appreciate the first list before beginning the second."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "There are no constituents at this table tonight. No officials, either. Just neighbors sharing a holiday. I have to remind myself there's a difference sometimes."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "Seeing Pendrelle Manor filled with people again carries a certain significance. Restoring a building matters. Restoring the life inside it matters more."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "Mother has opinions regarding which holiday traditions have been observed correctly. Father has wisely positioned himself at the opposite end of the table."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "Most of my evenings are spent considering what Blackmere needs from me tomorrow. Tonight, I'd rather appreciate who's here with me now."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "There are moments a person shouldn't organize, schedule, or attempt to improve. A beautiful sky and someone worth sharing it with qualifies."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "I understand why people come here with someone special. It's difficult to remain guarded beneath a sky that refuses to be subtle."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "Helen told me recipes are not municipal ordinances and minor deviations are permitted. I maintain that clearly written instructions exist for a reason."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "Theo asked me to taste the dish instead of evaluating whether I'd followed every step correctly. An unexpectedly difficult instruction."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "It's useful being the student occasionally. People are remarkably patient when you're willing to admit you don't know something."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "I normally cross the Square before most of Blackmere is awake. Seeing everyone gathered at this hour makes the town feel entirely different."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "I brought my portfolio with me this morning. Frederick took one look at it and placed it behind the counter. Apparently I'm permitted to retrieve it after breakfast."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "Most mornings begin with a list of problems requiring attention. Starting one with conversation and a warm meal instead feels almost irresponsible. Almost."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Beatrice - A

Dialogue: "Jonas has expressly prohibited town business at the table. Claudia has agreed to enforce the rule. I appear to have been outvoted before supper even began."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-beatrice-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Beatrice - B

Dialogue: "The Mercers have a way of making responsibility feel communal. Someone notices what needs doing, someone else does it, and nobody requires recognition afterward. Blackmere could learn from that."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-beatrice-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Beatrice - C

Dialogue: "I spend most days carrying Blackmere's problems into every room I enter. Around this table, people seem determined not to let me. I'm grateful for that...even if I'm still learning how to allow it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-beatrice-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Beatrice Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "Bought a new set of work gloves from one of the traders. Nothing fancy. Good stitching, thick leather. That's enough for me."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "Kira wants to see every stall. Milo wants something from every stall. Jonas and I should've planned for this."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "Met a miner from overseas selling ore I'd never handled before. We spent ten minutes comparing mines. Best conversation I've had all day."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "Milo thinks I can't catch him because he's small. He's forgetting who taught him to run through these woods."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "Kira hit me square in the back with a full bucket. Good throw. Still getting her back for it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "I work underground most days. Sun, trees, cold water...could be worse ways to spend an afternoon."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "Mine's built to solve a problem. If the judges like it, good. If they don't, it'll still solve the problem."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "Spent enough years hauling ore by hand to know where a machine could save somebody's back. That's where I start."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "Kira keeps telling people which invention is mine. Loudly. Suppose I've got my own advertising department now."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "Fields are finished. Pantry's full. Kids are fed. Jonas is smiling. That's a good season."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "I wasn't here for every day of the harvest. Jonas handled more than his share while I was at the mines. Tonight's as much his as anyone's."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "Everyone keeps offering to clear the table. Normally I'd already be doing it. Jonas told me to sit down, so...I'm sitting."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "You spend enough time underground, darkness stops bothering you. Deep water at night? That's different."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "Milo asked if there's a monster in Graythorne Lake. Told him no. Nora told him maybe. Nora and I will be talking later."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "Kira wants to investigate every strange noise around the lake. She's brave. I'd prefer she were brave somewhere I can see her."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "I know what it's like waiting underground and wondering if the people above know you're all right. During the flood, too many families never got that answer."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "After the flood, I went back to work as soon as I could. Thought providing for my family was the best thing I could do. Maybe it was. Doesn't mean it was the only thing they needed."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "I keep Kira and Milo close on days like this. They probably think I'm hovering. They're right."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "Jonas has the fields planned. Kira has opinions about those plans. Milo has opinions about everything. Growing season's officially started."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "Nothing in the ground yet. Months of work ahead. I like that. You know exactly what needs doing."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "I told Jonas I'd handle the garden after brunch. He reminded me he's perfectly capable of handling his own garden. Fair point."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "Another year finished. Family's here. Roof's still standing. Could've gone worse."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "Milo's been waiting all evening for midnight. He'll make it another hour, then fall asleep five minutes before it happens. I'd put money on it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "I don't make resolutions. If something needs changing, I change it. Still...there are a few things I could do differently next year."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "Kids are warm, everyone's eaten, and nobody has anywhere they need to be. That's what a holiday ought to look like."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "I offered to help with supper. Then the dishes. Then the firewood. I've been told I'm not allowed to volunteer for anything else."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "Pendrelle Manor's different with this many people inside. Warmer. Not just from the fireplaces."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "Jonas and I don't get many quiet evenings. Work, farm, children...there's always something. Makes you appreciate one when it comes."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "Pretty view. Better company. That's about as poetic as you're getting from me."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "People make romance complicated. Sometimes it's just knowing who you want standing next to you when something beautiful happens."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "I cook at home. Feed four people often enough, you learn. Helen still found three things I've been doing wrong for years."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "Theo told me to stop rushing. Food won't cook faster because I'm impatient with it. Annoying advice. Accurate, though."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "If I learn one meal the kids like enough to stop arguing over dinner, I'll consider today productive."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "Breakfast I didn't have to cook before work. That's enough of a festival for me."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "Kira and Milo are eating. Jonas has coffee. Nobody needs anything from me. Not sure what I'm supposed to do with my hands."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "Usually I'm halfway to Ashfall by now. Sitting here with my family instead...I could stand to do this more often."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Claudia - A

Dialogue: "Yes, it's our house. No, that doesn't mean you're helping with the dishes. Sit down."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-claudia-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Claudia - B

Dialogue: "Jonas cooked, Kira helped set the table, and Milo claims he supervised. They handled it without me. Seems they do that more often than I give them credit for."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-claudia-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Claudia - C

Dialogue: "This table's taken plenty of wear over the years. Spilled drinks, homework, arguments, celebrations...wouldn't trade a mark on it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-claudia-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Claudia Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Edward - A

Dialogue: "One can learn a remarkable amount about a place by examining what its people choose to make well. I have always considered the Trade Fair as much an exchange of craftsmanship as an exchange of goods."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Edward - B

Dialogue: "Several traders have visited Blackmere before, [Player]. It is gratifying to see them return. A merchant may arrive once out of curiosity; returning suggests we have given them reason to remember us."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Edward - C

Dialogue: "I have acquired a rather fine tea from one of the merchants, [Player]. Entirely for the household, naturally. The fact that I intend to sample it personally is merely responsible quality assurance."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Edward - A

Dialogue: "I was assured that spectators would remain reasonably dry. I confess I questioned that promise the moment I saw the young Milo carrying a bucket."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Edward - B

Dialogue: "There is something rather wonderful about seeing Blackmere's young people allowed to be thoroughly undignified for an afternoon. I shall endeavor not to spoil the occasion by appearing too responsible."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Edward - C

Dialogue: "You appear to be enjoying yourself, [Player]. Good. A life filled entirely with productive endeavors would be admirably efficient and terribly dull."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Edward - A

Dialogue: "Ms. Pendrelle believed invention was at its finest when ingenuity and usefulness met somewhere in the middle. I suspect she would have found today's competition quite compelling."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Edward - B

Dialogue: "The finished invention receives the applause, but I find myself wondering about everything that preceded it. The abandoned designs, the failed attempts, the hours no one witnessed. Those are part of the accomplishment as well."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Edward - C

Dialogue: "Competition has its place, certainly. Still, I hope every contestant leaves proud of having contributed something of their own creation. There are achievements a score cannot adequately measure."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Edward - A

Dialogue: "The Mercers have provided generously for Blackmere throughout the season, [Player]. It seems only proper that tonight we celebrate the people behind the harvest rather than merely what their fields produced."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Edward - B

Dialogue: "I offered Mr. Mercer my assistance before supper. He informed me, quite firmly, that I was a guest. I admit I have never been especially accomplished at that particular role."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Edward - C

Dialogue: "There is a particular satisfaction in seeing a table filled by months of honest work. Not simply with food, mind you, but with the people who helped make the season worthwhile."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Edward - A

Dialogue: "Graythorne Lake has accumulated a considerable collection of unsettling tales over the years. Ms. Pendrelle knew several. She was remarkably skilled at withholding the ending until precisely the wrong moment."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Edward - B

Dialogue: "I do not consider myself particularly superstitious, [Player]. Nevertheless, if something should call your name from the middle of Graythorne Lake tonight, I would recommend against answering. There is no sense being impolite to common sense."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Edward - C

Dialogue: "Moonlight has a peculiar talent for making perfectly ordinary places feel unfamiliar. Perhaps that is why evenings such as this have kept Blackmere's old stories alive for so long."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Edward - A

Dialogue: "Service teaches one to remember people through small details. How they took their tea. Which chair they preferred. The sound of their footsteps approaching a room. Those are the things I find myself remembering today."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Edward - B

Dialogue: "There were people lost in the flood whom I had known for decades, [Player]. I have never found much comfort in saying they are gone. I find considerably more in ensuring they are remembered."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Edward - C

Dialogue: "Blackmere rebuilding does not mean Blackmere has forgotten, [Player]. I believe those two things must remain true together. We owe the departed our remembrance, and the living a future."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Edward - A

Dialogue: "A planted seed is a rather modest thing upon which to place so much expectation. Perhaps that is precisely why the beginning of the season deserves celebrating."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Edward - B

Dialogue: "The manor gardens taught me long ago that one cannot hurry growing things merely by worrying over them. An excellent lesson. I cannot claim to have mastered applying it elsewhere."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Edward - C

Dialogue: "There is considerable work waiting for everyone after today. All the more reason to enjoy the table while it is full and the season remains entirely ahead of us."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Edward - A

Dialogue: "I have witnessed many years begin in this Square. Different faces, different hopes, different worries...and yet that final anticipation before midnight remains remarkably unchanged."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Edward - B

Dialogue: "Ms. Pendrelle rarely made resolutions, [Player]. She maintained that if one knew what needed doing, waiting until the new year was simply procrastination dressed for the occasion."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Edward - C

Dialogue: "Another year ending once made me think primarily of duties completed and preparations ahead. These days, I find myself thinking more about the people with whom the year was shared."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Edward - A

Dialogue: "For many years, my concern on evenings such as this was whether Pendrelle Manor was prepared to receive its guests. Tonight, hearing these rooms filled with conversation again...I believe the manor has given me my answer, [Player]."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Edward - B

Dialogue: "I have been informed, repeatedly, that I am not to spend the entire holiday serving everyone. I have therefore limited myself to checking the dining room only twice. Thus far."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Edward - C

Dialogue: "A house does not become a home because it is immaculate. Ms. Pendrelle understood that better than I did. Looking around tonight, I believe I finally understand what she meant, [Player]."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Edward - A

Dialogue: "There are certain sights made more memorable by the person standing beside you. One discovers, with age, that the companionship often outlasts the memory of the view itself."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Edward - B

Dialogue: "One needn't make a grand declaration simply because the sky has provided a dramatic backdrop. Sometimes remaining beside someone, quietly and willingly, says considerably more."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Edward - C

Dialogue: "Romance is often described in extraordinary terms, [Player]. In my experience, its truest measure is rather ordinary: discovering that someone's presence has become the place in which you are most at ease."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Edward - A

Dialogue: "I have prepared meals when necessity required it, though I would never presume to call myself a cook. Watching Helen and Theo work reminds me that competence and mastery are very different things."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Edward - B

Dialogue: "A well-written recipe resembles good household instruction: precise enough to guide you, but never capable of accounting for every circumstance. Experience must supply the remainder."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Edward - C

Dialogue: "Theo suggested I needn't arrange every ingredient before beginning. I assured him that while I respect his expertise, there are limits to the sacrifices I am prepared to make for culinary education."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Edward - A

Dialogue: "There is a pleasant difference between preparing breakfast for a household and being invited to sit down while someone else prepares it. I confess the latter still feels rather indulgent."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-edward-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Edward - B

Dialogue: "Frederick runs this dining room with admirable ease. Of course, genuine hospitality only appears effortless because someone has already considered a hundred details before the guests arrive."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-edward-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Edward - C

Dialogue: "Martin used to rise before me when he was young and insist upon helping with breakfast. His contributions were rarely efficient. I find I remember them more fondly than the mornings when everything went according to plan."

Event: Festivals

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Edward - A

Dialogue: "The Mercers possess a style of hospitality quite unlike my own. Considerably louder, somewhat less orderly, and every bit as effective."

Event: Festivals

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Edward - B

Dialogue: "Mrs. Mercer caught me collecting empty plates and returned one to my place before I had taken three steps. I recognize a direct order when I receive one."

Event: Festivals

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Edward - C

Dialogue: "There is a great deal of affection around this table, though very little of it requires saying aloud. Meals prepared, chairs pulled closer, someone remembering what another person prefers...I have always thought care reveals itself most clearly in such details."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-edward-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Edward Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Elise - A

Dialogue: "Three days of increased trade brings obvious benefits, provided people don't mistake novelty for value. I've already seen several purchases I suspect will be regretted by tomorrow."

Event: Festivals

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Elise - B

Dialogue: "The merchants seem pleased with the turnout. More importantly, several have expressed interest in returning. A successful fair is useful. A successful fair that creates future business is better."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Elise - C

Dialogue: "I intended to browse for twenty minutes. I've been here considerably longer. Apparently curiosity is capable of disrupting a perfectly reasonable schedule."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Elise - A

Dialogue: "Running through a forest while carrying an open bucket of water is inefficient on several levels. Everyone appears delighted by this."

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Elise - B

Dialogue: "Nora has been watching me for the past several minutes. She has a bucket. I dislike the implications."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Elise - C

Dialogue: "My original strategy was simply not to participate. That proved ineffective almost immediately."

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Elise - A

Dialogue: "An invention should justify the resources required to build it. If the problem costs less to tolerate than the solution costs to maintain, I remain unconvinced."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Elise - B

Dialogue: "I've revised my invention four times. The first version worked. The fourth works reliably. There is a significant difference."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Elise - C

Dialogue: "Competition makes some people take unnecessary risks. I prefer arriving with something thoroughly tested. It is less dramatic, certainly. I can live with that."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Elise - A

Dialogue: "A strong harvest gives Blackmere more than a full pantry. It provides stability for the months ahead. I find that considerably easier to celebrate."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Elise - B

Dialogue: "Jonas refuses to discuss next season tonight. Sensible. I have several questions, but I'm exercising restraint."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Elise - C

Dialogue: "Good weather helped. So did preparation, experience, and months of work. I prefer celebrations that acknowledge the difference between fortune and effort."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Elise - A

Dialogue: "There is almost certainly a rational explanation for every unusual sound coming from Graythorne Lake tonight. 'Almost' is doing more work in that sentence than I would prefer."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Elise - B

Dialogue: "I don't believe Graythorne Lake is haunted. I also see no benefit in testing that conclusion by wandering along the shore alone."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Elise - C

Dialogue: "Nora has provided six different explanations for what supposedly lives beneath this lake. The fact that they contradict one another has not reduced her confidence."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Elise - A

Dialogue: "The flood proved how quickly years of careful preparation can become insufficient. I dislike admitting that. It remains true."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Elise - B

Dialogue: "There were families who lost homes, livelihoods, savings...and people. I could help calculate what rebuilding would cost. There were losses no ledger could represent."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Elise - C

Dialogue: "I prefer problems with solutions. Today is a reminder that some things cannot be repaired. We remember them instead."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Elise - A

Dialogue: "A new growing season begins with considerable uncertainty. Weather, yields, pests, market conditions...people seem remarkably cheerful considering the variables."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Elise - B

Dialogue: "Jonas prepares for what he can control and accepts what he cannot. I've always admired that distinction. I'm considerably better at the first half."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Elise - C

Dialogue: "Nothing has been harvested yet, and we're already celebrating. Ordinarily I'd question that logic. Today...I think I understand it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Elise - A

Dialogue: "I review my finances at the end of every year. My goals as well. The second exercise is considerably less cooperative."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Elise - B

Dialogue: "People speak about the new year as though midnight erases uncertainty. It doesn't. Still...a clear point from which to begin again has value."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Elise - C

Dialogue: "My instinct is to plan the coming year before this one has ended. Tonight, I'm attempting something different. I'm leaving tomorrow until tomorrow."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Elise - A

Dialogue: "Large holiday gatherings are inherently unpredictable. Seating changes, people arrive late, conversations overlap...and somehow none of that seems particularly important once everyone is here."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Elise - B

Dialogue: "Pendrelle Manor is well suited to an evening like this. Plenty of space, warm fires, everyone together. Efficient, certainly. Pleasant too."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Elise - C

Dialogue: "I usually prefer knowing exactly how an evening will proceed. Tonight I haven't checked the time once. I'm choosing to regard that as progress."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Elise - A

Dialogue: "There is no practical reason someone's presence should change how beautiful the aurora appears. And yet...it does."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Elise - B

Dialogue: "I understand why people choose someone special to accompany them here. A memorable evening is one thing. Knowing exactly who you wanted beside you for it is another."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Elise - C

Dialogue: "Trusting someone has always seemed like accepting a risk you cannot properly calculate. Perhaps that's the point. Some decisions aren't improved by knowing the odds."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Elise - A

Dialogue: "Helen's instructions are precise. Measurements, temperatures, timing. I appreciate a discipline in which 'approximately' can usually be avoided."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Elise - B

Dialogue: "Theo told me to judge the mixture by texture rather than time. I asked how long that normally takes. Apparently I missed his point."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Elise - C

Dialogue: "My first attempt wasn't correct. My second was. A fifty-percent success rate sounds much better when there are only two attempts."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Elise - A

Dialogue: "My mornings are normally structured to the minute. Today there is nowhere I need to be until breakfast ends. It's an unusual amount of freedom."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Elise - B

Dialogue: "Frederick has already offered me more coffee twice. I declined the second cup. I am reconsidering that decision."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Elise - C

Dialogue: "There is something reassuring about seeing everyone gathered before the day's problems begin. Perhaps that's pessimistic. I meant it positively."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Elise - A

Dialogue: "The Mercers never seem concerned with whether a gathering proceeds according to plan. Food appears, people talk, someone laughs too loudly, and the evening succeeds anyway. Curious."
