ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-elise-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Elise - B

Dialogue: "I asked whether I should bring anything. Jonas said myself. That is an exceptionally vague contribution, but he seemed satisfied."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-elise-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Elise - C

Dialogue: "There is no measurable return on an evening spent around a table with people you care about. I used to think that made such things difficult to evaluate. It may simply mean evaluation is unnecessary."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-elise-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Elise Moreau

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "Trade Fair days always remind me of the Inn at its busiest. New faces, old stories, people carrying things from places I've never seen...half the fun is finding out what brought everyone here in the first place."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "I've already invited three traders to stop by the Inn before they leave. One mentioned a tea blend I've never tried, another knows a stew recipe from the coast, and the third just looked tired. That's reason enough."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "You can always tell which visitors have been traveling too long. They stop looking at the merchandise and start looking for somewhere to sit. I keep wanting to bring chairs over from the Inn."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "Felix warned me that Milo had a bucket and a plan. I should have taken that more seriously."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "I'm trying not to spend the whole afternoon reminding everyone to drink water, watch their footing, and come dry off if they get cold. Helen says that's missing the point of the festival."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "Honestly, it's good seeing Felix run around with everyone. He used to hesitate before joining anything this loud. Now I can barely keep track of where he went."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "I built mine because I kept thinking about all the little things that pull a host away from the room. If an invention lets me spend more time actually talking to people, that's useful to me."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "Some of these inventions are incredible. I keep looking at them and thinking, 'Could that help at the Inn?' Helen has asked me to stop mentally renovating the place during judging."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "Win or lose, anyone willing to put something they made in front of the whole town deserves a good meal afterward. Nervous work burns through a person's appetite."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "I offered to help Jonas with the food. He reminded me this is his supper, not mine. So now I'm sitting here watching other people serve things, which is apparently supposed to be relaxing."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "A meal like this tastes better when you know how much work came before it. Every dish has months behind it. Makes you slow down a little."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "Felix went back for another helping without me asking whether he'd eaten enough. That's growth for both of us, I think."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "Graythorne Lake is beautiful at night, but I admit the stories don't help. I heard one earlier about something tapping beneath a boat. I could've lived quite happily without that detail."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "Felix is fascinated by every strange sound out here. I'm trying to encourage the curiosity without encouraging him to investigate any of it alone."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "If you get cold, I've got an extra blanket. If you get hungry, I packed something. If a lake monster appears...well, I admit I didn't prepare for everything."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "After the flood, the Inn became a place where people came because they didn't know where else to go. I kept thinking food and a warm room might make things easier. Sometimes it did. Sometimes all we could do was sit with them."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "There are people I still expect to see walk through the Inn door. Strange how the mind keeps a place ready for someone long after they're gone."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "I used to think helping meant knowing what to say. I've learned better. Sometimes remembering someone with another person is enough."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "There’s something comforting about Seedwake. Everyone knows there’s work ahead, but for one morning we get to celebrate the possibility of what might grow."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "I brought a little extra food. Jonas told me not to. I brought it anyway. Hosting may be contagious."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "Felix has already wandered off twice to look at something near the fields. I keep telling myself curiosity is good for him. The difficult part is letting him go look."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "End of another year already. I always think I'll remember every face that came through the Inn, every meal, every conversation. Then the years pile up. Maybe that's why evenings like this matter."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "Helen told me I'm not allowed to leave the dance to check on the Inn. Theo agreed with her. Felix agreed with both of them. I appear to have lost this vote."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "My resolution? Rest more. That's what Helen says mine should be, anyway. I was going to say 'make the Inn even better,' but apparently that proves her point."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "This is the sort of gathering I love most. Plenty of food, plenty of people, nowhere anyone needs to rush off to. Pendrelle Manor feels made for it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "I've checked whether everyone has a drink three times. Edward has checked four. Between the two of us, nobody in this manor stands a chance of being neglected."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "Helen keeps reminding me I'm a guest tonight. That's harder than it sounds. When someone asks where the extra plates are, every instinct I have says get up."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "Helen and I have seen plenty of beautiful nights together. Funny thing is, I remember less about what the sky looked like and more about what she said while we were watching it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "There's a reason people bring someone special up here. A view like this is lovely on its own, sure, but sharing it makes it feel like the night belongs to both of you."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "You spend enough years with someone and romance changes shape. Less about grand gestures, more about still wanting them beside you whenever something beautiful happens."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "You'd think running an inn would make me immune to being corrected in a kitchen. Helen has disproven that theory several times already today."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "Theo and I have argued about this recipe for years. Not seriously, mind you. Mostly seriously. Today we finally made both versions side by side. Helen says neither of us won."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "Best part of teaching someone a recipe is watching them stop checking the instructions and start trusting themselves. Worst part is when they improve it and you have to admit it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "Now, before you say anything: yes, I've eaten. Helen made me sit down before anyone arrived. Apparently serving breakfast on an empty stomach is 'exactly the problem.'"

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "Look at this room. Every table full, everyone talking over each other, Felix somewhere in the middle of it all...this is exactly what I always hoped the Inn would be."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "I keep trying to refill cups before people ask. Theo keeps pushing me back toward my chair. We're both very committed to our positions."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Frederick - A

Dialogue: "The Mercers host differently than we do. Less fussing, more shouting across the table, and somehow everybody gets exactly what they need. I admire it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-frederick-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Frederick - B

Dialogue: "Jonas told me to stop helping. Claudia told me to stop helping. Kira physically moved the serving bowl away from me. I'm beginning to detect a pattern."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-frederick-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Frederick - C

Dialogue: "I spend so much time trying to make sure everyone else feels welcome that I forget how good it feels when someone does the same for me. The Mercers are rather good at reminding me."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-frederick-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Frederick Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Helen - A

Dialogue: "I found a merchant selling spices I've never cooked with before! I only meant to buy one or two, but then he started explaining what each one was used for and...well, Frederick is going to need another shelf."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Helen - B

Dialogue: "The best part of the Fair is asking traders what they miss eating when they're away from home. You learn so much about a place from the meal someone wishes they could have again."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Helen - C

Dialogue: "I've already invited two merchants to the Inn for supper. They're traveling alone, and nobody should spend three days in Blackmere without at least one proper meal."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Helen - A

Dialogue: "Felix tried sneaking past me with a bucket behind his back. Sweet boy. Terrible liar."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Helen - B

Dialogue: "I've packed towels, drinks, snacks, and a change of clothes for Felix. Frederick says that's too much. Frederick also asked me where the towels were ten minutes ago."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Helen - C

Dialogue: "Go on, get soaked! Clothes dry. Hair can be fixed. A good summer memory is worth the trouble."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Helen - A

Dialogue: "My invention isn't glamorous, but neither is trying to keep three pots warm while four people need something from you at once. If it makes the kitchen easier, I'm proud of it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Helen - B

Dialogue: "I keep looking at all these inventions and thinking about the people behind them. Nobody spends that much time making something unless some part of them really believes it matters."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Helen - C

Dialogue: "The judges will choose what they choose. I'm more interested in whether everyone remembers to eat today. Nervous contestants are terribly easy to forget about."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Helen - A

Dialogue: "Look at this table! Pumpkin, potatoes, fresh bread, everything grown or made nearby...you can practically taste the whole season in one meal."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Helen - B

Dialogue: "I offered to bring something, of course. Jonas told me I was absolutely forbidden from cooking for someone else's supper. I brought preserves. Those don't count."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Helen - C

Dialogue: "Harvest meals always make me happy. Not because the food is fancy. Because every dish says, 'We made it through another season. Sit down and share it with us.'"

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Helen - A

Dialogue: "I brought something warm to drink. Graythorne Lake is beautiful after dark, but spooky stories are much easier to enjoy when your hands aren't freezing."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Helen - B

Dialogue: "Felix keeps pretending the stories don't bother him. Every time something splashes in the lake, he moves about half a step closer to us."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Helen - C

Dialogue: "Theo told one of those horrible lake stories when we were children and I couldn't sleep for two nights. He's still far too pleased with himself about it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Helen - A

Dialogue: "After the flood, I cooked because it was something I knew how to do. Soup, bread, whatever people could manage. It didn't fix anything, but sometimes a warm bowl was enough to help someone through another hour."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Helen - B

Dialogue: "There are recipes in my notebook tied to people who aren't here anymore. I used to avoid making some of them because it hurt. Now I think cooking them is one way I keep those people at the table."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Helen - C

Dialogue: "You don't have to be strong today. You don't have to make anyone else feel better, either. Just be here. That's enough."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Helen - A

Dialogue: "I love this meal because everything still feels possible. Nobody knows which crop will thrive or what the season will bring, so we might as well start with something delicious."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Helen - B

Dialogue: "I've already started thinking about what I'll cook with the first harvest. I know, I know, we've barely planted anything. Let me be excited."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Helen - C

Dialogue: "Seeds always remind me of recipes. You begin with something small, give it time and care, and eventually it becomes something you can share with someone else."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Helen - A

Dialogue: "I've been baking for tomorrow since sunrise. Frederick made me stop an hour ago and said the kitchen would survive without me. I'm choosing to trust him."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Helen - B

Dialogue: "Every year I think about what I want to do better next year, and somehow the answer is always 'more.' Maybe this time I'll try making room for a little less."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Helen - C

Dialogue: "Midnight's lovely, but I think my favorite part comes earlier. Everyone gathering close, finding who they want beside them, making sure nobody starts the new year alone."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Helen - A

Dialogue: "I tried offering to cook tonight. Edward looked at me, Frederick looked at me, Theo looked at me...apparently everyone had already agreed I wasn't allowed."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Helen - B

Dialogue: "A holiday meal doesn't need to be perfect. Something can burn, somebody can arrive late, the table can be too crowded...if everyone feels wanted, you've done it right."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Helen - C

Dialogue: "I love seeing Pendrelle Manor like this. Every room warm, people talking everywhere, someone always looking for another chair. That's what a home should sound like."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Helen - A

Dialogue: "Frederick and I have shared plenty of beautiful evenings, but I still like standing beside him when the sky does something extraordinary. Some things never stop feeling special."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Helen - B

Dialogue: "There's something wonderfully simple about tonight. No meal to prepare, no room to tidy, nowhere anyone needs me to be...just a beautiful sky and someone worth staying close to."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Helen - C

Dialogue: "People think romance has to be grand. Honestly? Sometimes it's just noticing that after all these years, they're still the first person you want to turn toward when something beautiful appears."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Helen - A

Dialogue: "All right, remember: taste as you go! A recipe can tell you amounts and timing, but it can't tell you whether your tomatoes were sweeter this year or your herbs came in stronger."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Helen - B

Dialogue: "Theo and I learned this recipe together when we were young. Mine changed over the years. His did too. Neither one is exactly what we started with anymore, and I think that's part of what makes family recipes special."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Helen - C

Dialogue: "Don't worry if yours doesn't look like mine. I'm serious! If it tastes good and you learned something, then you've made it properly enough for today."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Helen - A

Dialogue: "I know this is supposed to be a shared breakfast, but watching everyone enjoy something I cooked is half the fun for me. Yes, I ate too. Frederick checked."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Helen - B

Dialogue: "Felix always asks for the same thing first, then ends up trying whatever somebody else orders. I pretend not to notice so he can feel adventurous."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Helen - C

Dialogue: "This room is exactly how I like it: plates everywhere, people talking too loudly, someone asking for more jam, and absolutely no empty chairs."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Helen - A

Dialogue: "Claudia told me I wasn't bringing food tonight. I respected her wishes completely. I brought dessert."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-helen-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Helen - B

Dialogue: "I love meals at this house. Nobody worries about being proper, somebody's always reaching across someone else, and the children tell stories faster than the adults can follow them."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-helen-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Helen - C

Dialogue: "You know what makes a family meal special? It isn't the recipe. It's knowing who likes the crispy pieces, who wants extra gravy, who'll pretend they don't want dessert and steal a bite from someone else. That's the part you can't write down."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-helen-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Helen Holt

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Irene - A

Dialogue: "The Square sounds different during the Fair. More voices. More footsteps. The bell still cuts through all of it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Irene - B

Dialogue: "Some merchants return every year. Others come once and vanish down another road. Traditions make room for both."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Irene - C

Dialogue: "I purchased nothing. I listened. That was sufficient."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Irene - A

Dialogue: "I have spent decades maintaining brass mechanisms. I see no reason to volunteer for a soaking."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Irene - B

Dialogue: "The children have been told not to splash me. I expect that instruction to survive another five minutes."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Irene - C

Dialogue: "They are loud. Wet. Completely undisciplined. They also seem happy. I suppose that is the intention."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Irene - A

Dialogue: "A mechanism should perform the same task the hundredth time as it did the first. Novelty is secondary."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Irene - B

Dialogue: "Mine began with a simple question: could the chimes keep time without constant attention? The answer required rather more work."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Irene - C

Dialogue: "There is dignity in making something that will still function after its maker is gone."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Irene - A

Dialogue: "Harvest suppers mark the year more reliably than most calendars. Planting. Waiting. Gathering. Then this."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Irene - B

Dialogue: "The same meal never tastes quite the same twice. Different season. Different people. That is not necessarily a flaw."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Irene - C

Dialogue: "The work is finished. For tonight. People should learn to recognize when that is enough."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Irene - A

Dialogue: "Graythorne Lake is older than every story told about it tonight. That should be unsettling enough."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Irene - B

Dialogue: "Sound carries strangely over dark water. A bellwarden notices such things."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Irene - C

Dialogue: "James enjoyed these stories more than I did. He claimed fear made people pay attention. On nights like this, I almost agree."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Irene - A

Dialogue: "A bell marks what has passed. It cannot call it back. I learned that distinction years ago."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Irene - B

Dialogue: "Names should be spoken while someone still remembers the voice that answered to them."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Irene - C

Dialogue: "We owe the dead remembrance. We owe the living more than remembrance."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Irene - A

Dialogue: "The first planting has always mattered. Not because success is assured. Because beginning despite uncertainty matters."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Irene - B

Dialogue: "Another season begins. People call that repetition. I prefer continuity."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Irene - C

Dialogue: "Seeds require patience. Unfortunately, patience cannot be hurried either."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Irene - A

Dialogue: "I have rung the bell at the turn of many years. The sound is familiar. The moment never is."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Irene - B

Dialogue: "People count down as though time requires encouragement. Midnight will arrive precisely when it intends to."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Irene - C

Dialogue: "James used to stand below the tower when I rang the new year in. I still look for him. Habit is stubborn."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Irene - A

Dialogue: "Pendrelle Manor has hosted holidays for generations. The decorations change. The reason people gather does not."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Irene - B

Dialogue: "I nearly declined tonight. Crowded rooms have never suited me. I am...glad I reconsidered."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Irene - C

Dialogue: "Tradition survives because people return to it willingly. Obligation alone would not keep it alive this long."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Irene - A

Dialogue: "James and I watched the aurora once from the tower balcony. I remember very little of the sky. I remember him."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Irene - B

Dialogue: "People choose their company carefully tonight. Sensible. Beautiful moments have a way of attaching themselves to whoever shared them."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Irene - C

Dialogue: "Do not spend the entire evening wondering whether the person beside you feels the same. Time is rarely improved by hesitation."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Irene - A

Dialogue: "A recipe survives only when someone bothers to teach it properly. Memory is not an adequate archive."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Irene - B

Dialogue: "Theo said timing is everything. At last, a cooking instruction with some sense."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Irene - C

Dialogue: "Helen altered an old recipe and improved it. I dislike admitting that sentence. It remains true."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-irene-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Irene - A

Dialogue: "I am usually halfway through my morning duties by now. Sitting through breakfast feels almost extravagant."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-irene-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Irene - B

Dialogue: "Frederick remembers how I take my tea. I have never told him. That man pays too much attention."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-irene-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Irene - C

Dialogue: "The town bell sounds different from inside the Inn. Softer. Perhaps distance does that to more than sound."

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Irene - A

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Irene - B

Dialogue: "I prefer quiet meals. This is not a quiet meal. I have survived."

Event: Festivals

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Irene - C

Dialogue: "Families create traditions without realizing it. Same seats. Same stories. Same arguments. Years later, those ordinary habits are what remain."

Event: Festivals

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Irene Calder

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "Have you been around the whole Square yet? Because you have to see everything! I met a trader this morning who came from so far away she'd never even heard of Blackmere before. By the time we finished talking, I'd invited her to the farm."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "I told myself I wasn't buying anything today. Then I found something for Kira, something Milo would love, something Claudia could use...apparently shopping for other people doesn't count."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "This is what I love about the Trade Fair! Half these people were strangers this morning, and now everyone's swapping stories and recommending stalls to each other. Give Blackmere a few hours. We'll adopt anybody."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "Don't you dare trust Kira! She gave me the sweetest little smile five minutes ago, and then dumped an entire bucket over my head. I was so proud."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "Milo and I have an alliance. A very serious, very strategic alliance. If he turns that bucket toward me, however, all agreements are immediately void."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "You're still dry! How did that happen? No, no, we have to fix this. You can't come all the way out here and leave looking respectable."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "Mine handles some of the everyday ranch work. Feeding, checking supplies, keeping things organized...basically all the jobs that somehow multiply the moment you turn your back."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "I love seeing what everybody makes because it's such a strange little window into how their mind works! Give thirty people the same pile of parts and somehow you end up with thirty completely different ideas."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "Are you nervous? Don't be! Actually, that's terrible advice. Be nervous if you're nervous. Just don't let it convince you that what you made isn't worth showing."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "Look at everybody! Jonas is pretending he isn't proud, Claudia actually sat down before everyone else finished eating, the kids are already asking about dessert...this is a good night."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "There's always this moment at the end of harvest when the farm suddenly feels quiet. Months of rushing around, then one evening everyone just...sits. I think I need that part more than I realize."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "Please tell me you've eaten enough. Wait, don't answer that, I can see your plate. You haven't. Stay right there."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "All right, I know Graythorne Lake isn't actually full of horrible creatures waiting to drag us under. Probably. But if something touches my ankle tonight, I'm abandoning every one of you."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "Kira wants to investigate the lake, Milo wants to hear every scary story twice, and Claudia looks about three seconds away from taking both of them home. I love this family."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "You know what makes a frightening story worse? When everyone gets quiet afterward and you suddenly notice how dark the lake is. So! Let's keep talking."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "I always want to make people feel better. Today makes me remember that sometimes there isn't a better to make. Sometimes you just stay beside someone."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "After the flood, I kept trying to get everyone at the farm smiling again. Meals, stories, anything I could think of. It took me a long time to understand that they were allowed to be sad."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "If today is difficult for you, you don't need to pretend otherwise around me. I mean that. We can talk, we can be quiet...whatever you need."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "This might be my favorite morning on the farm! Fresh air, everybody together, a whole growing season ahead of us...how are you supposed to look at all that possibility and not get excited?"

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "Kira already has predictions for what'll grow best this year. Milo mostly wants to know when he gets to eat everything. Both approaches have merit."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "Nothing's grown yet, nothing's gone wrong yet, and everyone still thinks this might be the best harvest we've ever had. I know that won't last forever. Let me enjoy it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "Come midnight, I want everybody cheering! Yes, everybody. I will personally locate anyone hiding around the edge of the Square and drag them into this celebration if necessary."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "I love looking around right before the year changes. You start remembering everything that happened, who came into your life, who stayed...oh, now I'm getting sentimental."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "My resolution is to worry less about whether everyone else is having a good time. Which is why I've only asked six people whether they're enjoying themselves tonight. That's improvement!"

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "This is exactly what winter needs. Big table, warm house, too many conversations happening at once, and enough food that everyone insists they couldn't possibly eat another bite right before dessert."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "I've been checking on people all evening. Then Helen caught me and asked whether *I* was having a good time. Can you believe that? Completely unfair use of my own tactics."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "Pendrelle Manor feels so alive tonight! I swear, every time I walk into another room there's somebody laughing. You should be proud of this place."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "Theo mentioned he was coming tonight. Not that I asked! Well...I did ask. But only because I was asking everyone. I mean, I wasn't, but I could have been."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "Everyone says Aurora Watch is supposed to be romantic, which is silly. You can enjoy a beautiful sky with anybody! A friend, family, Theo...I don't know why I said Theo specifically."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "Do you think Theo likes the aurora? He probably does. Most people do. I could ask him. That would be completely normal, wouldn't it? Don't look at me like that."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "Helen taught me years ago that recipes are supposed to be shared, and I love that! Somebody teaches you, then you make it for somebody else, then they learn it...it's like feeding people and telling a story at the same time."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "Theo is much more serious about this than Helen is. It's kind of adorable when he starts explaining why something has to be done exactly...ah. I meant impressive. Very professional. Anyway!"

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "I already know I'm taking this recipe home and changing something. Helen expects it. Theo will complain. Then he'll taste it. We have a system."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "I love breakfast here because nobody has fully decided what kind of day they're having yet. Give them something warm, a little conversation, maybe a second cup of coffee...you can practically watch the morning improve."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "I was going to sit with Jonas and Claudia, then I saw Irene by herself, then Felix waved me over, then Helen asked me something...I've changed seats four times. That's a successful breakfast."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "Theo makes a wonderful breakfast, doesn't he? I mean, Helen does too! Frederick too. Everybody does. This is a very talented family. That's all I meant."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Jasmine - A

Dialogue: "Welcome! You're going to eat too much, Kira will ask you at least twelve questions, Milo will interrupt half your answers, and somebody will absolutely start an argument across the table. You'll fit right in."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-jasmine-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Jasmine - B

Dialogue: "I know we see each other every day, but I still love having everyone around this table. Jonas, Claudia, Kira, Milo...ordinary nights with them are some of my favorite memories."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-jasmine-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Jasmine - C

Dialogue: "Nobody leaves hungry tonight! And I don't only mean food. Talk to somebody you haven't talked to enough. Tell a story. Stay longer than you planned. That's what these dinners are for."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-jasmine-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jasmine Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "Trade Fair always makes the Square feel bigger somehow. Same buildings, same streets, but suddenly there are people here carrying pieces of places most of us have never seen."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "Kira's been asking traders where they came from. Milo's been asking what they brought. Jasmine's been asking both. I've mostly been trying to keep up."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "Bought a few seeds from a merchant this morning. Don't know how they'll take to Blackmere soil yet, but there's only one good way to find out."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "Milo told me he had a foolproof plan. Kira told me he absolutely did not. I've decided to stay out of that argument."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "I spend enough summer days wishing for water while I'm working the fields. Seems wrong to complain about getting some today."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "Claudia's pretending she's taking it easy. Kira's already planning an ambush. Give it five minutes."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "I built mine because watering the same rows by hand year after year teaches you exactly where the work could be easier."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "A good invention reminds me of a good farm tool. You stop noticing how clever it is once you realize how much easier it makes the work."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "The contestants look nervous. Can't blame them. Growing something and showing something you've made have one thing in common: eventually, you have to let other people see what came of the work."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "This is the part of the season I wait for. Not finishing the harvest. Sitting down afterward and seeing everyone who helped make it happen."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "People keep thanking me for the meal. Truth is, there are too many hands behind this table to count properly."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "Claudia's finally sitting down. That's how I know the harvest is actually over."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "Graythorne Lake is calm tonight. That's usually when people decide it must be hiding something."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "Kira wants to investigate the shoreline. Milo wants to find a monster. Claudia wants both of them where she can see them. I'm siding with Claudia."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "I don't put much stock in ghost stories. Still, if something calls from the middle of that lake, I think we can all agree not to answer."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "The flood left a mark on every family in Blackmere, whether you can see it or not. Some things grow around damage. Doesn't mean the damage was never there."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "I used to think keeping everyone steady meant keeping the hard parts to myself. I've learned silence can weigh on a family too."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "We remember the people we lost, but I think we should remember who they were before the flood too. A life deserves more than its ending."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "First planting always feels good. Empty rows, fresh soil, a whole season ahead. Nothing's certain yet, but that's never stopped us from starting."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "Kira wants to know exactly what'll grow best. Milo wants to know what'll grow fastest. I told them the same thing: give it time."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "People think farming starts when the seeds go into the ground. Truth is, most good seasons start long before that, with planning and patience."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "Another year gone. Fields changed, kids grew, half the things we worried about worked themselves out. The other half...well, we handled those too."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "Milo says next year is going to be his most adventurous one yet. Kira says she's going to discover something nobody else has. I believe both of them."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "I don't need the next year to be perfect. Just hope we keep talking when things are hard and laughing when they're not."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "A house full of people, good food, nowhere anyone needs to rush off to. Hard to ask much more from a winter holiday."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "Claudia keeps looking for something useful to do. I keep telling her sitting with us counts."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "Pendrelle Manor feels different when it's full like this. Less like something inherited. More like something being lived in."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "Claudia and I don't get many nights with nothing asking for our attention. When we do, I've learned not to waste them."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "Beautiful sky's worth seeing. Having someone beside you who makes you forget to look up for a while isn't bad either."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "Love isn't always the big moments people talk about. Most of it is ordinary days. Still nice to get an extraordinary one now and then."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "Helen told me I measure ingredients like I'm planting rows. She's not wrong. I like knowing where things are supposed to go."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "Theo says this recipe needs patience. That's fine by me. Most worthwhile things do."
