ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "Kira wants to change the recipe. Milo wants to eat it before it's finished. I suddenly understand why Helen sets rules."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "Most mornings I'm outside before breakfast's properly settled. Sitting here afterward feels almost like I'm forgetting something."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "Frederick keeps filling people's cups before they ask. Helen keeps checking the food. They make hospitality look effortless. It isn't."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "Kira's already talking about what she wants to do today. Milo's still deciding what he wants for second breakfast. Good to know everyone's on schedule."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Jonas - A

Dialogue: "Glad you came. Don't worry about helping with anything tonight. Sit down, eat, and let the house be noisy around you."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-jonas-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Jonas - B

Dialogue: "Kira's telling one story, Milo's telling another, Jasmine's somehow telling both louder than either of them. That's about normal for us."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-jonas-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Jonas - C

Dialogue: "Best thing about a full table isn't the food. It's looking around and knowing everyone there chose to stay awhile."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-jonas-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Jonas Mercer

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Julian - A

Dialogue: "International trade is useful for reasons far beyond commerce. Seeds, dyes, preserved specimens, unfamiliar medicinal preparations...each stall is effectively a traveling archive of regional knowledge, though I suspect most visitors are more interested in the prices."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Julian - B

Dialogue: "I encountered a merchant selling dried fungi from a climate considerably warmer than ours. He identified three by common name. I identified two incorrectly before examining the gills. A useful reminder not to trust first impressions."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Julian - C

Dialogue: "People assume unfamiliar goods are inherently rare. Sometimes they are. Sometimes they are merely common somewhere else. Scarcity is often a matter of geography rather than importance."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Julian - A

Dialogue: "Repeated exposure to cold water on a warm day is unlikely to cause harm in healthy adults. That is not permission to empty a bucket over me."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Julian - B

Dialogue: "The children have transformed the forest into a remarkably effective study in pursuit behavior, concealment, and poor judgment."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Julian - C

Dialogue: "Kira asked which route through the trees would minimize her chance of being seen. I gave her an answer. In retrospect, perhaps I should not have provided tactical consultation."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Julian - A

Dialogue: "An invention is most interesting when one can identify the precise problem that forced its existence. Remove the problem, and much of the design suddenly makes sense."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Julian - B

Dialogue: "My own entry is not particularly dramatic. It presses, preserves, and protects botanical samples. That may sound mundane until you lose a specimen collected during a season that will not return for another year."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Julian - C

Dialogue: "The judges will naturally assess function and ingenuity. I am more interested in whether the inventor understood the variables affecting failure. Reliable work begins there."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Julian - A

Dialogue: "The variety on this table tells us quite a bit about the season: soil condition, rainfall, crop resilience, storage success. A meal is data, if one knows how to read it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Julian - B

Dialogue: "People celebrate the harvest as though the work ends here. Biologically speaking, nothing has ended. Seeds are retained, soil begins recovering, perennials enter dormancy. The cycle has merely changed phase."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Julian - C

Dialogue: "Jonas asked whether I liked the meal. I began explaining the nutritional value of several ingredients. He clarified that a simple 'yes' would suffice."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Julian - A

Dialogue: "Graythorne Lake produces several perfectly ordinary nocturnal sounds that become difficult to identify after dark. Waterfowl, frogs, fish breaking the surface...uncertainty supplies the rest."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Julian - B

Dialogue: "The human mind is exceptionally talented at assigning familiar shapes to poor visual information. Shadows become figures, ripples become movement, branches become limbs. Fascinating, really."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Julian - C

Dialogue: "I do not believe a monster inhabits Graythorne Lake. I also see no scientific value in wandering into deep water at night to prove it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Julian - A

Dialogue: "Catastrophe leaves records in more places than archives. Tree scars, soil deposits, altered riverbanks, damaged root systems...nature remembers events even when people wish it would not."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Julian - B

Dialogue: "I documented the environmental damage after the flood because documentation was something I could do. It took me longer to understand that people sometimes needed my presence more than my notes."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Julian - C

Dialogue: "Memory is an unreliable instrument, but that does not make it unimportant. Records preserve facts. People preserve meaning. We require both."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Julian - A

Dialogue: "Seed germination is an astonishingly complex process frequently reduced to 'put it in the ground and wait.' Accurate, technically. Insufficient, scientifically."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Julian - B

Dialogue: "This stage interests me more than harvest. Every seed contains potential, certainly, but also requirements: temperature, moisture, dormancy conditions, soil chemistry. Hope alone is not a growing medium."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Julian - C

Dialogue: "Milo asked whether speaking encouragingly to seeds makes them grow faster. I told him there is no reliable evidence. He is conducting his own trial."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Julian - A

Dialogue: "Humans are fond of assigning significance to arbitrary divisions of time. Midnight arrives every evening. Tonight we applaud it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Julian - B

Dialogue: "I review my journals at year's end. The mistakes are often more instructive than the successful observations, though considerably less pleasant to reread."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Julian - C

Dialogue: "Another year adds another layer to a long study. One should be careful not to become so occupied recording life that one neglects to participate in it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Julian - A

Dialogue: "Crowded winter gatherings have practical advantages. Shared heat, shared food, reduced travel...of course, people generally object when I describe holidays in those terms."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Julian - B

Dialogue: "Pendrelle Manor contains several plant specimens in its older decorative work that are no longer commonly cultivated. I have been instructed not to spend the entire evening examining them."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Julian - C

Dialogue: "Traditions are often treated as though their value lies in remaining unchanged. That is rarely true in nature. Survival usually requires adaptation."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Julian - A

Dialogue: "The aurora is produced by charged particles interacting with the upper atmosphere. A precise explanation. It does not explain why people instinctively stand closer together while watching it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Julian - B

Dialogue: "There are observations best recorded with instruments. Others become meaningless if you spend the entire moment measuring them. Tonight may belong to the second category."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Julian - C

Dialogue: "People often remember who stood beside them during unusual natural events. Emotional association is remarkably persistent. I would choose one's company carefully."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Julian - A

Dialogue: "Cooking is applied chemistry disguised as domestic routine. Heat alters proteins, acids alter texture, moisture controls structure. Helen asked me not to explain this while people were eating."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Julian - B

Dialogue: "Theo instructed us to judge doneness by aroma and texture rather than strict timing. Irritatingly imprecise. Also correct."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Julian - C

Dialogue: "Recipes survive through repetition, but repetition without understanding invites mistakes. Learn why a step matters, not merely that it exists."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Julian - A

Dialogue: "There is a measurable improvement in my patience after breakfast. I have not formally studied the effect. The evidence is nevertheless compelling."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Julian - B

Dialogue: "Frederick asked whether I intended to bring my journal to the table. I told him I bring it everywhere. He asked whether I intended to open it. That was a more difficult question."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Julian - C

Dialogue: "Breakfast gatherings are efficient social environments. People are stationary, receptive, and generally less inclined to wander away before one finishes explaining something."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Julian - A

Dialogue: "Mercer Farms is an excellent example of interdependent systems. Crops feed animals, animals enrich soil, labor is distributed across the household...Jonas tells me this is not how normal people describe family supper."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-julian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Julian - B

Dialogue: "Kira asked me three botanical questions before I sat down. Milo asked whether mushrooms can think. Jasmine interrupted with a story unrelated to either subject. I am enjoying myself."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-julian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Julian - C

Dialogue: "I have spent decades preserving observations in journals. Evenings like this remind me that some knowledge survives differently: through habits, stories, recipes, and people teaching one another without ever calling it instruction."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-julian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Julian Hale

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Lena - A

Dialogue: "I like watching people discover something they've never seen before. For a moment, everyone looks a little younger."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Lena - B

Dialogue: "One of the traders brought pressed flowers from another region. Different shapes, different colors...yet somehow they still felt familiar."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Lena - C

Dialogue: "Anya has been quieter than Kira and Milo, but she's looking at everything. That's usually how I know she's enjoying herself."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Lena - A

Dialogue: "Anya told me she wasn't going to participate. She's currently hiding behind a tree with a bucket. I'm choosing not to mention the contradiction."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Lena - B

Dialogue: "I used to worry that enjoying days like this meant I was forgetting something important. It doesn't. Joy doesn't erase anything."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Lena - C

Dialogue: "If you're planning to splash me, at least have the courtesy to look guilty beforehand."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Lena - A

Dialogue: "There's something brave about showing people something you made yourself. Even if they judge the work, some part of you is standing beside it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Lena - B

Dialogue: "My fountain began as something simple for the gardens. I suppose that's often how meaningful things start: small, useful, and cared for."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Lena - C

Dialogue: "The scores will matter today. Years from now, I suspect people will remember what these inventions did for them instead."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Lena - A

Dialogue: "Harvest always makes time feel visible. You remember the empty fields, then look at this table and realize how much happened in between."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Lena - B

Dialogue: "Anya seems comfortable here. That means more to me than whether she finishes everything on her plate."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Lena - C

Dialogue: "There is a gentleness to celebrating work once it's finished. No rushing toward the next thing. Just gratitude for what made it through."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Lena - A

Dialogue: "Graythorne Lake is peaceful during the day. Tonight, every reflection seems to belong to something just beyond sight."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Lena - B

Dialogue: "Anya says she isn't frightened. I believe her. I also noticed she hasn't wandered very far from me."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Lena - C

Dialogue: "Not every old story needs to be explained away. Sometimes a little mystery teaches us to pay closer attention."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Lena - A

Dialogue: "Ian's name is one among many here today. I think that's important. My grief is personal, but the loss belongs to all of Blackmere."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Lena - B

Dialogue: "I used to come here thinking remembrance meant holding tightly to every detail. Now I think love survives even when some of those details soften."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Lena - C

Dialogue: "If you remember someone today, remember something that made you smile too. They were more than the day you lost them."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Lena - A

Dialogue: "I like beginnings that don't ask us to forget what came before. A new season can grow from old soil."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Lena - B

Dialogue: "Anya chose a seed to watch this year. She says she's curious whether it'll make it. I think she understands more about growing things than she realizes."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Lena - C

Dialogue: "Planting requires a kind of faith, doesn't it? You make room for something that isn't here yet."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Lena - A

Dialogue: "There was a time when the end of a year only made me think about everything that wouldn't come back. I'm trying to look forward now too."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Lena - B

Dialogue: "Anya has changed so much this year. Sometimes I notice it all at once and wonder how I missed it happening."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Lena - C

Dialogue: "I think I'll dance tonight. That shouldn't feel like an important decision...but somehow it does."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Lena - A

Dialogue: "There are people I wish could see Pendrelle Manor like this. I can miss them and still be grateful for everyone filling these rooms now."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Lena - B

Dialogue: "Anya has wandered off with the other children. I used to feel uneasy whenever I couldn't see her. Tonight, I think I'll let her enjoy herself."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Lena - C

Dialogue: "A warm home isn't measured by how many rooms it has. It's whether people feel they're allowed to stay."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Lena - A

Dialogue: "Ian and I shared nights beneath the stars when we were younger. Remembering that doesn't make tonight belong any less to the people standing here now."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Lena - B

Dialogue: "There's something tender about choosing one person to share a view like this with. You aren't only saying, 'Look at the sky.' You're saying, 'I wanted you here when I saw it.'"

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Lena - C

Dialogue: "For a long time, I thought loving someone after loss would mean making less room for the person who came before. I don't believe the heart works that way anymore."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Lena - A

Dialogue: "Helen has a lovely way of teaching. She corrects you without making the mistake feel embarrassing. People learn better when they feel safe enough to be wrong."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Lena - B

Dialogue: "Recipes carry memories so easily. One smell, one familiar taste, and suddenly you're standing in a kitchen from years ago."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-lena-b

Options: None

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Lena - C

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Lena - A

Dialogue: "Breakfast feels wonderfully ordinary. I mean that as a compliment. There are times when ordinary life is exactly what you hope to get back."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-lena-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Lena - B

Dialogue: "Anya is talking more than usual this morning. I won't draw attention to it. Some things grow better when you don't hover over them."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-lena-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Lena - C

Dialogue: "Frederick has a gift for making people feel expected rather than merely invited. There's a difference."

Event: Festivals

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Lena - A

Dialogue: "The Mercers have always made room for Anya without treating her as though she needs special handling. I'm more grateful for that than I've ever properly said."

Event: Festivals

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Lena - B

Dialogue: "Kira gets Anya talking. Milo gets her laughing. Jonas and Claudia give her somewhere safe to do both. That's a good family."

Event: Festivals

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Lena - C

Dialogue: "I used to think making new memories would crowd out the old ones. Evenings like this have taught me otherwise. There seems to be room for both."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-lena-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lena Thorne

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Martin - A

Dialogue: "Three days brings a great many visitors through Blackmere. It is good to see the Square busy again."

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Martin - B

Dialogue: "I spoke with a trader who remembered visiting Blackmere years ago. He seemed pleased by what he found this time."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Martin - C

Dialogue: "There is more to carry home than what fits in a parcel. I suspect the traders understand that better than most."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Martin - A

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Martin - B

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Martin - C

Dialogue: "I considered leaving before someone noticed I was still dry. I waited too long."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Martin - A

Dialogue: "My entry organizes letters for distribution. Simple work benefits from being done well."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Martin - B

Dialogue: "Reliability is more important to me than spectacle. An invention should work when someone depends upon it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Martin - C

Dialogue: "Everyone here entrusted something they made to the judges. That requires a certain courage."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Martin - A

Dialogue: "The Mercers have earned a quiet evening. Whether they are capable of having one is another matter."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Martin - B

Dialogue: "It is easy to notice a harvest once it reaches the table. Easier to overlook the months that brought it here."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Martin - C

Dialogue: "I offered to help clear the table. I was told, quite firmly, to remain seated."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Martin - A

Dialogue: "Graythorne Lake is unusually quiet tonight. I am undecided whether that improves matters."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Martin - B

Dialogue: "I don't believe every story told about this lake. I see no reason to challenge them, either."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Martin - C

Dialogue: "If you intend to walk farther along the shore, take a lantern. Superstition is no excuse for poor judgment."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Martin - A

Dialogue: "There are messages I carried before the flood whose recipients never had the chance to answer. I remember their names."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Martin - B

Dialogue: "Father taught me that remembering someone needn't always be solemn. Mother taught him that first."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Martin - C

Dialogue: "Some words never get said in time. I try not to assume there will always be another opportunity."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Martin - A

Dialogue: "Beginning something requires accepting that you cannot guarantee the outcome. I respect farmers for that."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Martin - B

Dialogue: "The fields look empty now. Everyone else seems able to see what they might become."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Martin - C

Dialogue: "There is work waiting after brunch. For once, none of it is mine. I am attempting to accept that."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Martin - A

Dialogue: "I have delivered many resolutions written in letters. People are often more honest when addressing their future selves."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Martin - B

Dialogue: "Father tells me I should make fewer plans for next year. I suspect he knows I will ignore that advice."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Martin - C

Dialogue: "I am accustomed to measuring a good year by what was accomplished. Perhaps who shared it deserves equal consideration."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Martin - A

Dialogue: "Father looks at home here. I don't think he realizes how noticeable the difference is."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Martin - B

Dialogue: "I offered to assist with the evening. Father declined. I suspect he considers making me remain a guest a form of assistance."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Martin - C

Dialogue: "My mother believed holidays were meant to interrupt ordinary responsibilities. I understood the principle better when I was a child."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Martin - A

Dialogue: "Choosing who accompanies you tonight seems more significant than choosing where to stand. The view is visible from either place."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Martin - B

Dialogue: "A few sincere words can stay with someone for years. A night like this seems an appropriate time to say them."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Martin - C

Dialogue: "There is comfort in knowing someone chose to stand beside you when they could have stood anywhere."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Martin - A

Dialogue: "Theo's instructions are precise. I appreciate that."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Martin - B

Dialogue: "Helen told me to stop checking the recipe after every step. I have not yet complied."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Martin - C

Dialogue: "There is satisfaction in making something for another person. Cooking and correspondence have that much in common."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Martin - A

Dialogue: "I usually eat before my route begins. Sitting this long afterward feels unfamiliar."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Martin - B

Dialogue: "Frederick noticed my cup was empty before I did. I understand where Felix learned to pay attention."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Martin - C

Dialogue: "Most mornings are spent moving from one door to another. It is pleasant having everyone come to the same place for once."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Martin - A

Dialogue: "The Mercers leave little opportunity for silence. I mean that more fondly than it sounds."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-martin-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Martin - B

Dialogue: "I asked whether anything needed carrying. Jonas handed me a plate and told me to carry that to the table for myself."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-martin-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Martin - C

Dialogue: "I am accustomed to being useful when someone makes room for me. The Mercers make room first and ask nothing afterward. I am still learning what to do with that."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-martin-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Martin Ashcroft

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Roland - A

Dialogue: "There was a time when three unfamiliar merchants arriving in Blackmere would've been noteworthy. Look at the Square now. I'm rather glad we outgrew that."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Roland - B

Dialogue: "Agatha has already informed two traders that their prices are unreasonable. Neither seemed prepared for the negotiation that followed."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Roland - C

Dialogue: "Every visitor takes a little knowledge of Blackmere away with them. Best make certain we give them something worth remembering."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Roland - A

Dialogue: "I was informed that my age grants me immunity from being splashed. I have lived long enough to recognize a lie when I hear one."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Roland - B

Dialogue: "Watch the children carefully. Years from now, they'll remember who chased them through these trees far better than they'll remember who won."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Roland - C

Dialogue: "Agatha says I'd be foolish to join in. She is correct. Fortunately, foolishness and old age aren't mutually exclusive."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Roland - A

Dialogue: "Judging becomes harder each year. Not because the inventions are difficult to understand, mind you. Because I know how much hope can be sitting behind one little score."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Roland - B

Dialogue: "A clever invention catches my attention. A useful one keeps it. Blackmere was built by people solving ordinary problems well."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Roland - C

Dialogue: "Don't build what you think an old judge wants to see. Show us what you believe should exist. That's far more interesting."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Roland - A

Dialogue: "A full table after harvest is an old pleasure. Tired hands, good food, and everyone promising they'll never work that hard again."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Roland - B

Dialogue: "The Mercers have done fine work this season. Be sure to tell them so. Gratitude does more good when it's spoken."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Roland - C

Dialogue: "I've attended enough harvest suppers to know the rule: never take your first serving too seriously. Someone will insist you have another."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Roland - A

Dialogue: "Graythorne Lake had frightening stories attached to it when I was young too. Different monsters, mostly. Apparently even legends change with the times."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Roland - B

Dialogue: "Agatha claims she isn't interested in ghost stories. Yet she corrects anyone who tells one incorrectly."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Roland - C

Dialogue: "A dark lake, an old tale, and somebody willing to listen. That's all people have ever needed to frighten one another properly."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Roland - A

Dialogue: "I have watched Blackmere lose people before. You never replace them. You simply keep building a town they would still recognize as home."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Roland - B

Dialogue: "Some of the names remembered today belonged to people I knew for decades. Others I barely knew at all. Their absence matters just the same."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Roland - C

Dialogue: "Honor the people we lost. Then tomorrow, care for the people still here. Remembrance ought to teach us something about living."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Roland - A

Dialogue: "I've planted enough seeds to know optimism is part of the work. You put something tiny into the earth and behave as though a harvest is perfectly reasonable."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Roland - B

Dialogue: "Young trees used to stand where some of Blackmere's oldest orchards are now. Give things enough care and time, and one day you forget they were ever small."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Roland - C

Dialogue: "A new season doesn't ask whether the last one went according to plan. It arrives and gives you another patch of ground to work with."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Roland - A

Dialogue: "I remember when Beatrice could barely stay awake until midnight. Now she's the mayor welcoming Blackmere into another year. Time has a sense of humor."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Roland - B

Dialogue: "Agatha and I have welcomed quite a few new years together. She still tells me I'm counting down too quickly."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Roland - C

Dialogue: "Don't burden the new year with too many promises tonight. Leave yourself enough room to be surprised by it."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Roland - A

Dialogue: "Pendrelle Manor has seen many winters. Seeing these rooms full again is a better restoration than fresh timber alone could ever provide."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Roland - B

Dialogue: "Agatha and I have spent enough holidays together that we no longer exchange many surprises. I find I prefer the certainty."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Roland - C

Dialogue: "A tradition doesn't belong to the people who began it. It belongs to whoever cares enough to carry it forward."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Roland - A

Dialogue: "When Agatha and I were young, I spent half an evening beneath the aurora trying to think of something impressive to say. She eventually told me to be quiet and enjoy it with her."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Roland - B

Dialogue: "People make romance terribly complicated when they're young. After enough years together, you learn that being the person someone still wants beside them is rather extraordinary on its own."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Roland - C

Dialogue: "If there's someone you hoped would be here tonight, don't spend the whole evening watching them from a distance. The sky will give you something to talk about."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-aurora-watch-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Roland - A

Dialogue: "Helen says recipes should change a little with each cook. Theo says some instructions exist for a reason. Between the two of them, we may actually get supper."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Roland - B

Dialogue: "Learning from someone younger than you is good practice. Anyone who reaches my age believing they've finished being taught has wasted part of the journey."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Cooking with the Twins - Roland - C

Dialogue: "Agatha has requested that I bring home whatever we learn today. Whether she trusts me to prepare it is another question."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-cooking-with-the-twins-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Roland - A

Dialogue: "Breakfast has a way of making conversation easier. Perhaps people haven't had enough time to prepare their defenses yet."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-roland-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Roland - B

Dialogue: "I've watched generations gather around tables at this Inn. Different faces, same arguments over who gets the last helping."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Breakfast at the Inn - Roland - C

Dialogue: "Frederick has built the sort of place where people linger after they've finished eating. That's usually a sign you've done something right."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-breakfast-at-the-inn-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Roland - A

Dialogue: "Listen to this house for a moment. Stories, laughter, somebody interrupting somebody else...that's the sound of a family comfortable with one another."

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Roland - B

Dialogue: "Kira asked me what Blackmere was like when I was her age. I gave her the short version. We would have missed supper otherwise."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-roland-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Roland - C

Dialogue: "I helped build parts of this town. These days, I think I'm prouder of the families who filled it than anything we built with our hands."

ID: dialogue_festivals-supper-with-the-mercers-roland-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Roland Whitmore

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Theo - A

Dialogue: "I always come home from the Trade Fair with something for the bakery. New spice, unusual flour, some recipe I've never heard of...Helen says I'm very predictable."

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Theo - B

Dialogue: "A trader let me try a pastry from his hometown this morning. I've been thinking about the filling ever since. That's usually how a new recipe starts."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-theo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Blackmere Trade Fair - Theo - C

Dialogue: "It's nice having so many unfamiliar faces around. Give them a day or two and somebody from Blackmere will know their whole life story."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-blackmere-trade-fair-theo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Theo - A

Dialogue: "Felix promised he wouldn't splash me. Very sincere about it too. That's how I knew I shouldn't believe him."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-theo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Theo - B

Dialogue: "I thought about staying somewhere dry and watching. Then somebody handed me a bucket. Hard to argue with that."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-theo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Fun In The Sun Festival - Theo - C

Dialogue: "Everyone will go home soaked, tired, and hungry. That last part I know how to fix."

ID: dialogue_festivals-fun-in-the-sun-festival-theo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Theo - A

Dialogue: "My inventions usually begin with something that bothers me in the bakery. Dough proving unevenly, too many pastries to finish by hand...nothing grand. Just problems worth solving."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-theo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Theo - B

Dialogue: "Some of these inventions could change Blackmere. Mine might help somebody make better bread. I'm comfortable with that."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-theo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Theo - C

Dialogue: "I used to wonder whether I ought to invent something more impressive. Then I remembered I actually like what I make."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-grand-showcase-theo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Theo - A

Dialogue: "There's something satisfying about eating together after the harvest. You can taste the season everyone just spent working through."

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-theo-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Theo - B

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-theo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Theo - C

ID: dialogue_festivals-harvest-supper-theo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: C

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Theo - A

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Theo - B

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-theo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Theo - C

Dialogue: "I brought something warm to eat. Scary stories are easier to enjoy when your hands aren't freezing."

ID: dialogue_festivals-hollow-moon-night-theo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Theo - A

Dialogue: "Food was one of the first things the Inn could offer people after the flood. Nothing special. Just something warm when they needed it."

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Theo - B

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Theo - C

Dialogue: "I don't have anything wise to say about loss. I think being here for one another is enough."

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-theo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Theo - A

Dialogue: "Fresh bread and the beginning of planting season belong together somehow. Both start with very little and need patience before they're ready."

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Theo - B

Dialogue: "I like this part of the year. Everybody has plans, the fields are waiting, and nobody knows exactly how things will turn out yet."

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-theo-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Theo - C

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Theo - A

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Theo - B

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Theo - C

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-theo-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Theo - A

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Theo - B

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Theo - C

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Theo - A

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Theo - B

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Aurora Watch - Theo - C

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: C

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Theo - A

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Theo - B

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Supper with the Mercers - Theo - C

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Theo Bennett

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Order Number: 1

Variant: B

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: C

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: B

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Order Number: 1

Variant: A

Order Number: 1

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Walter - C

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: A

## Festivals - Harvest Supper - Walter - B

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: B

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: C

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Walter - A

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: A

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Walter Pierce

Variant: A

Variant: B

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Lucian/Vivian - A

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Lucian/Vivian - B

Dialogue: "I have checked my entry repeatedly. There comes a point when further adjustment ceases to improve the work. Recognizing that point is...something I continue to practice."

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: B

## Festivals - Grand Showcase - Lucian/Vivian - C

Dialogue: "Myrtle never praised an invention merely for being complicated. If three gears accomplish what twelve were doing, she would remove nine and ask why you wasted her time."

## Festivals - Hollow Moon Night - Lucian/Vivian - A

Dialogue: "Graythorne Lake possesses an impressive collection of legends. Contradictory ones, naturally. Consistency has never been a requirement for a good ghost story."

Dialogue: "People behave differently when they cannot see what lies ahead of them. They slow down. Listen carefully. Stay near one another. Perhaps uncertainty has its uses."

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: B

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Lucian/Vivian - A

Dialogue: "I spent a long time after the flood thinking only about what might have prevented it. Eventually, one must accept that understanding the past and controlling it are not the same thing."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-lucian-vivian-a

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Lucian/Vivian - B

Dialogue: "Myrtle taught me that a damaged mechanism should be studied before it is repaired. People are not mechanisms. It took me longer than it should have to understand the distinction."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-lucian-vivian-b

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - River Remembrance Day - Lucian/Vivian - C

Dialogue: "We cannot return to the hour before everything changed. I would not trust anyone who promised otherwise. What we can do is decide what the hours afterward become."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-river-remembrance-day-lucian-vivian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: C

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Lucian/Vivian - A

Dialogue: "Planting is an admirable exercise in patience. You perform the work correctly, provide what you can, and then accept that the outcome is no longer entirely yours to determine."

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Lucian/Vivian - B

Dialogue: "A seed cannot be hurried because one finds waiting inconvenient. There are processes that function precisely because we allow them the time they require."

Event: Festivals

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Seedwake Brunch - Lucian/Vivian - C

Dialogue: "I understand the appeal of this festival better than I expected. Beginning something without knowing exactly how it will end is uncomfortable. It is also unavoidable."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-seedwake-brunch-lucian-vivian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: C

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Lucian/Vivian - A

Dialogue: "I maintain the Bell Tower throughout the year, yet tonight everyone waits for the same toll. Familiar sounds acquire remarkable significance when people agree they matter."

Event: Festivals

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: A

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Lucian/Vivian - B

Dialogue: "At midnight, nothing mechanical changes. The clocks continue as they always have. We are the ones who decide the moment marks a beginning."

Event: Festivals

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Snow Bell's Eve - Lucian/Vivian - C

Dialogue: "I used to consider resolutions rather inefficient. If something requires changing, why wait for a particular date? I may have underestimated the value of choosing a moment together."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-snow-bells-eve-lucian-vivian-c

Options: None

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: C

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Lucian/Vivian - A

Dialogue: "Myrtle's work remains throughout this house if one knows where to look. I once believed preserving that legacy meant ensuring nothing changed. I am less certain of that now."

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Variant: B

---

## Festivals - Toll of Hearths - Lucian/Vivian - C

Dialogue: "A tradition repeated without thought becomes routine. A tradition people continue choosing has meaning. There is a considerable difference."

Event: Festivals

ID: dialogue_festivals-toll-of-hearths-lucian-vivian-c

Order Number: 1

Speaker: Lucian/Vivian Darrow

Dialogue: "If you came here hoping to share tonight with someone, I suggest spending less time anticipating the right moment. Some moments become right because you chose to act within them."
